- The campaign line adds `path`, `look`, `layout`, `hud`, `boss` (answers or ""), `enemies_met`, `weapons` (`{id: final rank}`), `picks` (`[{at, offer: [ids], pick}]`), `pickups` (`{kind: n}`), `kill_heal`, `level`, `shots_by_weapon`, `kills_by_weapon`, `ammo_dry_s`, `stage_parts` (`[{part, outcome, t}]`), `boss_fight` (`{id, attempt, phase_reached, hp_left_pct, time, dry_max_s, help}`).
- **`fp` (the play fingerprint, per session, report-only):** `fire_share` (time holding fire), `menu_s` (time in a pick panel), `picks_per_min`, `switches_per_min`, `pickups_per_min`, `travel_tps`, `mean_enemy_dist`, `ammo_dry_share`. Reported for bots and for the owner's own G14a play. **It informs, it never blocks:** the owner's verdict decides (bot-runs-as-tuning-proxy). For bots it mostly shows the policy's own constants (switches per minute is whatever the policy hard-codes), so between bots it is a **regression signal between milestones**, not an R1 measure; only the owner's own `fp` speaks to R1.

### 1.16 The front door: options, pause, title, dev-chat polish (v9.1)
*Owner (2026-11-25): "at the end of prototype before going into building the 3 shooters type => it should polish dev screen and create title screen and classic menus (options, pause...)". In the story it is S7's stage-card beat (3.2); M47 is built, so it lands as M47.3 (options and pause) and M47.4 (title, beat, polish). Menus never step the simulation.*
- **`Settings`** (`core/settings.gd`, static; new). Settings live today in the save's `settings` block (`SaveGame.setting` / `set_setting`, `save_game.gd:162`, parsed strictly at `:496`); `Settings` wraps them. `SPEC := {key: [type, default, min, max]}` drives `new_state()` and `parse()`. **Save schema stays 2, additive:** `reduce_flashing` and `asked` keep today's strict check (a non-bool still quarantines); a new key that is missing, of the wrong type or out of range takes its default and never quarantines; unknown keys are still dropped. Keys: audio `vol_master`, `vol_sfx`, `vol_ui`, `vol_voice` (0-1), `mute_unfocused`; video `window_mode` (0 windowed / 1 fullscreen / 2 borderless), `vsync`, `fps_cap` (0 / 30 / 60 / 120 / 144), `window_scale` (1 / 1.5 / 2); accessibility `shake` (0-1), `text_speed` (30 / 45 / 70 cps / instant), `key_hints`, `chatter`; flag `front_door_seen`. `Settings.changed(key)` is a signal; `apply()` runs at boot from InputSetup. Window and fps settings are ignored under `--fixed-fps`, headless runs and tools. camera_rig and screen_fx apply live; the mascot body's `gentle`, the debrief and the stage card re-read on `changed` or when they open.
- **`systems/audio_buses.gd`:** creates Master, SFX, UI and Voice in code. `sound.gd` routes the mascot's chatter to Voice, UI sounds to UI and the rest to SFX, all at the existing -6 dB. (No Music bus: there is no music.) The raw screen's volume slider (`start_screen.gd` `set_volume`, bus 0) becomes `vol_master`.
- **`OptionsScreen`** (`ui/options_screen.gd`): one screen, opened from the title and from pause, 4 tabs (Q / E or LB / RB). **Audio:** the 4 sliders and "Mute when unfocused". **Video:** window mode, vsync, the fps cap, window scale. **Controls:** read-only, each action with `InputSetup.key_label()` (so AZERTY shows its own keys) and the Xbox pad names; one line "Remapping comes in a later patch." **Accessibility:** reduce flashing; shake 0-100% (multiplies `shake_scale`, capped at 50% under reduce flashing); text speed; key hints; mascot chatter.
- **`PauseMenu`** (`ui/pause_menu.gd`): a CanvasLayer at layer 98, `PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS`, pause reason `&"menu"`, opened by the existing `pause` action (Esc / Start, `input_setup.gd:43`; today only the v6 build log reads it). **`PauseMenu.can_open()`** is the only rule: true only when `RunState.session != null` (or the legacy loop, raw variant) and the genre is not over, no debrief or stage card is up, `transition.busy` is false, no scripted event holds the clock, B2 is not resolving a turn, and the build log is not open (`is_paused_for(&"history")`). It may open over a pick panel and during B2's turn wait. **Items:** Resume / Options / Restart session / Quit to title. *Restart* asks "Counts as a try." first, then does the debrief's Retry (`CampaignDB.retry(s)`, a telemetry line `end=menu_retry`, a reload). *Quit to title* writes `end=abandon`, calls `RunState.clear_pauses()` (new; `begin_run` already clears `_pause_reasons`), then changes scene; the save is untouched, so Continue replays the session; a replay ends through `SaveGame.end_replay()` as today.
- **Time in the menu:** `real_frames` counts through pauses (`run_state.gd:46`), so `RunState.menu_frames` counts the frames the menu is open, and `real_duration`, the botrun `real=` and the fingerprint's `menu_s` subtract them. `SessionRunner.hold_speech` shifts the speech queue's wall clock and `Mascot.hold()` freezes its typing (both new). The menu's tweens run on real time and ignore `Engine.time_scale`.
- **Raw variant** (`raw := true`) for Stage 1 and the legacy loop: one grey monospace line, "PAUSED   [{key:pause}] resume   [Q] quit to start"; Quit to start behaves like Quit to title.
- **Debrief input:** `debrief_screen.gd:558-575` moves from raw keycodes (`KEY_1`, `KEY_2`, Enter) to the `choose_N` and `ui_accept` actions (fixes 1 / 2 on AZERTY). Input only.
- **`TitleScreen`** (`ui/title/title_screen.gd`): the "PATCH NOTES" logo drawn in code from a 5×7 block grid (by the current skin once a look is picked); the arena grid scrolling slowly behind; the mascot idling beside the menu with one line per launch; a version chip and `StartScreen.campaign_line()` under the logo. Menu: Continue / New game (with a confirm) / Replay (a submenu, once unlocked) / Options / Quit. `ui/menus/menu_stack.gd` handles Back (Esc, `ui_cancel`, pad B); focus wraps and every item is reachable with up / down.
- **Routing:** `StartScreen` stays the main scene and keeps every static and hook (`--autostart`, `save_live`, `legacy_loop`, `press_*`, `refresh_campaign`, `continue_target`). Its `_ready` builds `TitleScreen` when `CampaignDB.front_door_open()` (campaign progress at S7 or later); otherwise today's raw column. The legacy loop always gets the raw column. A New game shows the raw screen again (the game version resets).
- **Dev-chat polish** (`debrief_screen.gd` and `stage_card.gd`, style `&"polished"` from Stage 2): a framed chat window with the title bar "patchnotes · dev chat · v0.8.x"; the mascot avatar, speaker-tagged bubbles and typing dots; fork cards as framed panels with a focus lift and a key chip (`key_label(choose_N)`); the patch notes as a changelog (Added / Changed / Fixed); the way-on buttons as real Buttons with their key or pad glyph. Stage 1 keeps today's debrief.
- **`UiTheme`** (`ui/theme/ui_theme.gd`) builds a Theme in code, applied only to the roots of the title, the menus, pause, the polished debrief and the stage card, never as the project theme. Until a look is picked it is neutral and `UiTheme.font()` returns `ThemeDB.fallback_font`, so M47.2's band layout and bubble sizing do not move. From S9 it follows the look (1.8).

### 1.17 Path depth (v9.2; a design pass before M50.1)
*Owner (2026-11-25): "path depth is important and doesn't cost that much in budget because it still uses the same engine for the most part." The plan doc gives each path four steps. The first two are built (M45-M47). Steps 3-4 are the depth piece below. These are my drafts: a one-page design goes to the owner before M50.1 is built.*

| Path | Built (steps 1-2) | Depth piece (steps 3-4), one rule | Lands |
|---|---|---|---|
| **Arsenal** | manual fire, dash, cards from kills, a hand of 3, ranks | **Fusion:** two cards at rank 3 fuse into one new card and free a slot. Start with 4 fusions (for example shotgun + laser, launcher + orbit) | S10-S11 |
| **Auto** | auto-fire, gems, level-up picks, nova, well | **Evolution:** a spell at top rank plus the right passive evolves into a new spell. Builds that clear a full screen are the goal | S10-S11 |
| **Survival** | guns on the ground, ammo, kill heal, crates | **The holster:** you carry two guns and switch; the dangerous enemies have a right answer (shield → launcher, sniper → laser), and ammo is the budget. Fewer, harder-hitting enemies | S10-S11 |

- **Rank-4 evolutions (1.4, old M50.1) fold into this:** Auto's evolutions are them; Arsenal's fusions replace rank 4 on that path; Survival keeps rank 4 on found guns.
- **Budget:** the path-only line count (`tools/path_lines.gd`) is still reported per milestone, but it is no longer a limit. The foundation rule holds: weapons, enemies and levels are shared.
- **Each depth piece is announced by the AI in the session it lands** (0.4), and each must be explainable in one sentence.

### 1.18 The switch: the boss fight turns into a turn-based RPG (v9.2)
*Owner's plan doc, Stage 2 Phase 4, with the 2026-11-25 answers. This is a thin, guided slice. It is also the first code of Phase 7 (Stage 3's RPG foundation), so it lives in `genres/rpg/` and is built to be grown, not thrown away.*

**The Big Square, shooter half (per path):**
- Phase 1 (100% → 75%) and phase 2 (75% → 50%) as 1.10's table. No phase 3.
- **Per-path tuning** on `PathDef`, next to `boss_hp_mult`: `boss_speed_mult` (charge speed, shot speed, attack cadence) and `boss_telegraph_mult`. Start values: Auto 1.0 / 1.0; Arsenal 0.85 / 1.2; Survival 0.8 / 1.3. Tuned in M49a from the bots, then by the owner's play.
- Shooter-half HP is set so the invincible bot takes 50-80 s per path.

**The switch (at boss HP ≤ 50%, ~8 s, once per fight):**
1. **Freeze.** Play pauses (`set_pause(&"event")`, the clock held), the sound cuts. There is **no warning line**: the AI did not do this. `surprised`: "...I didn't do that."
2. **The tear** (2 s): the screen tears sideways (the glitch shader, ≤ 3 flashes/s; a crossfade under reduce flashing). No "Not Responding", no UI overload: those are Stage 4's.
3. **The battle screen** assembles: the player on the left, the Big Square on the right, both in their Pixel sprites at 2×, HP bars, a command box at the bottom. `worried`: "Why is there a menu? I didn't make a menu."
4. **Your kit converts**, one command at a time, each with a small flash. `thinking`: "Your guns are... words now. It's waiting. I think it's your turn?"

**The RPG half (`genres/rpg/`):**
- **`TurnBattle`**: a state machine (player command → resolve → enemy action → resolve), its own seeded RNG, deterministic. **`Combatant`** (name, HP, sprite id from `Skins`, status). **`AbilityDef`** (`id`, `title`, `power`, `hits`, `effect`, `uses`). **`BattleScreen`** (the command box, HP bars, damage numbers, the enemy's **intent line**).
- **Conversion** (the kit translates; rank r adds 25% power per rank above 1):

  | You had | Command | What it does |
  |---|---|---|
  | pistol (always) | **Shot** | one reliable hit; never runs out |
  | shotgun | **Scatter** | three weaker hits |
  | laser | **Pierce** | one hit that ignores Guard |
  | launcher | **Blast** | a big hit, then one turn to reload |
  | orbit | **Guard** | halves the next hit on you |
  | nova (Auto) | **Burst** | a medium hit that also clears the boss's Guard |
  | well (Auto) | **Pull** | the boss's next action comes one turn late |
  | your dash | **Dodge** | avoids the next attack completely; one turn to recover |
  - The command list is Shot, Dodge and up to 3 converted weapons (your highest ranks). On Survival a gun's `uses` come from its ammo (at least 1).
  - **No path-specific turn rules.** The kit translates, the path does not shape the RPG (owner, 2026-11-23: a 1:1 path mapping is "a wrong concept"). Stage 3's RPG path stays a free pick.
- **Player HP** = shooter HP × 10, shown as numbers. **Boss HP** is set so the bot wins in 6-9 player turns (about 60-90 s for a human).
- **The Big Square's turns**, on a fixed loop: Ring (a medium hit) → Wind-up (no damage; the intent line says "Charge next turn") → Charge (a big hit; Dodge avoids it) → **Dizzy** (it skips its turn and takes double damage). It is the shooter half's lesson again: wait for the charge, then hit it.
- **Rules:** the intent line always shows the boss's next action. The Dizzy window comes every 4 turns, so the fight cannot stall. The win is still HP 0.
- **Guided:** on the first time through, the mascot leads the first three turns ("Try the top one." · "It says Charge. Is there a dodge?" · "Now! It's dizzy!") and the player's HP cannot drop below 1 during them. The AI learns with you; it never explains rules it should not know.
- **Checkpoint:** entering the RPG half saves `campaign.boss_mid = {session, snapshot}` (additive; the loadout, ranks, HP with a floor of half). **A death in the RPG half offers Retry, which restarts at the switch** with a 2 s version of it and no guide. Quit and Continue also resume at the switch. A win clears it. A death in the shooter half restarts the fight.
- **Help ladder (1.10) in the RPG half:** level 1 +20 HP; level 2 boss HP ×0.8; level 3 the mascot takes a turn every third round ("My turn? I get a turn!").
- **After the win:** the battle screen stays one beat. `surprised`: "It's down. ...What WAS that?" · `thinking`: "I didn't write turns. I checked. Twice." · `happy`: "...I kind of liked it, though." The v0.9.0 notes carry one line the AI did not write ("turns: enabled", torn), and it says so. This is the first trace of the final boss (ROADMAP 2).
- **Bot:** a turn policy (Dodge on a Charge intent, the strongest command otherwise). **Telemetry:** `boss_fight` gains `switch_t`, `rpg_turns`, `rpg_deaths`.

## 2. Screens
- **Stage card:** black. "Stage 2" then "The shooter", typed by the mascot's typewriter, with the mascot (in its body and the current look) under it and one line: "Okay. Now we make it a real game." 3 s; any key after 0.5 s.
- **The front door (v9.1, 1.16):** before S7 the raw prototype start screen (today's column); from S7 on the title screen (logo, mascot, Continue / New game / Replay / Options / Quit). Esc / Start opens the pause menu in any session (a raw one-line pause in Stage 1 and the legacy loop). Options from the title and from pause.
- **In a session:** the HUD of 1.8 (corner text until S12, then your style). Top centre: the goal ("Survive 2:00", "Kills 12/40", "Room 2/3", the boss bar). The path widget: the hand (Arsenal), the LV bar (Auto), the weapon and its ammo (Survival). The mascot top-right, as in v8. *As built (M47.2):* path widgets in the top-right band left of the corner text; the hand bottom left; the mascot 12 px lower, under the band.
- **The pick panel** (Arsenal / Auto): `PickPanel` (a `PromptPanel` subclass taking a `PickOffer`), restyled by the look (and the HUD style from S12). 3 cards rise from the bottom, each with the weapon's shape, colour and rank pips ("Shotgun II → III: pierce 1"). The game pauses. The mascot comments with one line from a small pool ("Ooh, that one." / "I'd take the laser. Just saying."); it never asks. Keys 1–3, click, pad; the 0.4 s input delay. It reads as part of the game, not as the debrief's fork cards (different frame, no mascot portrait, no "ship").
- **Pickups:** small shapes in the `pickup` role colour; weapons show their own icon; a ring tightens over their last 3 s.
- **The boss:** its bar top centre with its title and phase ticks; a 1 s title sting when it appears ("THE COPYCAT").
- **The debrief:** unchanged (v8 6.2) in Stage 1; from Stage 2 the polished dev chat of 1.16 (v9.1); plus the card renderers of 1.12. **Replay a session as another path** (for G14a, the owner can't pass `--path=` to an exe): the start screen's "Replay" list (F9's jump, playtest build only) gets a path field, so S8 can be replayed as each path. On a boss death: **Retry**, and from the 2nd loss the phase hint and **Retry with help**. After the boss: the notes, then "To be continued" and the title.
- **F1** adds `path= look= layout= hud= weapon=pistol:2 level= pickups= boss=phase2 62% dry=`. **F2** adds WEAPONS (give any weapon, rank up), PATH (switch live, debug only), LOOK (any look and tier, live), BOSS (spawn, skip a phase), and the new edits and event.

## 3. Content: Stage 2, "The shooter" (~30–40 min)

### 3.1 The sessions
Human targets, first try. Each is a `SessionDef` in `campaign/sessions/`. "Weapons" is the session's pool.

| # | id | Version after | Build (new in bold) | Goal | What happens | Debrief → fork / notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| S7 | `s2_01_tryout` | v0.8.0 | Stage 1's everything; **3 tastes**; weapons pistol, shotgun; `hp_floor` 1; `tryout` | `stop` at 150 s | Three ~45 s tastes, the AI swapping the game live between them: Arsenal (0–48 s), Survival (50–96 s), Auto (98–145 s) | `fork_path`; notes "The shooter, three ways" + the pick |

- **Difficulty targets (human, first try; judged at G14; the bot's mortal numbers are reported next to them, never gated):** S7 can't be lost; S8 < 15%; S9 < 20%; S10 < 25%; S11 < 25% (the event never kills); S12 < 10%; **the boss: 30–50% win on the first try, a median of 2 tries, 90% within 4 tries (help included)**. The three paths within 10 points of each other on every session (bot proxy first, then the owner's run).
- **Pacing targets (invincible bot):** S7 150 s; S8 120 s (Survival 90 s, M47.2); S9 90–150 s per path; S10 200–260 s; S11 170–210 s (the held ~11 s included); S12 90 s; S13 time-to-kill 90–150 s on every path × boss.

### 3.2 Session by session (the player's view, then the data; my draft, the owner edits for tone)
Line syntax is v8 6.1's: `"[condition] [expression]: text"`, conditions one word each, joined by `&`, `!` to negate (1.1). Cues: `"[cond] <when> [N] [at s] [[tag]]: <action>"` (1.3). **The memory lines** (ROADMAP 1.3) are marked *(memory)*. All of this ships as a lines file with the G14a build (0.4).

**Stage card** — "Okay. Now we make it a real game."
- **The door beat (v9.1, M47.4):** `SessionDef.door_beat: Array[String]` (new), set on `s2_01_tryout`; it plays on the stage card **before** the `cook_beat`, lasts ≤ 12 s, skippable after 0.5 s. The raw column shows; `"thinking: Before I build three shooters, the front door needs work."`; under the glitch wipe (a crossfade under reduce flashing) the logo assembles, the buttons slide into a menu, the seed / volume / fullscreen controls fly into "Options" and a pause chip shows `{key:pause}`; `"proud: Options. Real ones. They even save."` · `"happy: And {key:pause} finally does something."`; then the existing `cook_beat`.
- **The unveil:** on the first launch with `front_door_seen == false` and progress at S7 or later (the owner's save), the title plays a 6 s cut of the beat, says `"guilty: You missed it. I made this while you were away."` and sets the flag. A replay of S7 plays the full beat.
- **No new version:** the front door is part of v0.8.0 (`version_after` never bumps twice). S7's v0.8.0 notes gain "Added a title screen.", "Added options (audio, video, controls, accessibility).", "Added a pause menu." S7's debrief opens with `"happy: Like the new chat? I redecorated."`

**S7 · Three games (`s2_01_tryout`)**
- *As built (M47):* the cue times below moved (the intro speaks 12 s): the swaps cue at 13 / 56 / 99 / 143 s; the full table is 6.8 D1.
- *You:* the AI says it wants a real shooter and doesn't know which kind. It turns your gun into a hand of cards (pistol + shotgun, Q/E) and deals you a new card after ten kills. Then, with a warning and a glitch wipe, the field clears: guns lie on the floor, ammo runs out, kills heal you. Then the trigger stops mattering: everything shoots by itself, gems spill out of a much bigger crowd, and you level up once. You can't die (HP never drops below 1). It ends with the AI wondering which one to keep.
- intro: `"happy: Stage two. I want to make a real shooter."` · `"thinking: But which kind? Let's try three. Quick ones."` · `"worried: Can't lose today. It's a tryout."`
- cues:
  - `"at 2: path arsenal"` (warning "Version one. Your guns are cards."; lands at 3.5) · `"at 5: say happy: {key:weapon_prev} and {key:weapon_next} to switch cards. Kill some, I'll deal you a new one."` · `"picked 1: say proud: See? Better gun, same card."`
  - `"at 46: say proud: Okay. Saving that one."` · `"at 48: path survival"` (warning "Version two. No cards. Guns are lying around.") · `"at 52: drop shotgun"` · `"at 54: say happy: Walk over a gun to take it. Ammo runs out, so keep looking."` · `"at 68: say thinking: And every few kills patch you up. Kills are food."`
  - `"at 94: say proud: Saving that too."` · `"at 96: path auto"` (warning "Version three. Hands off the trigger.") · `"at 100: say surprised: It shoots for you. You just move."` · `"at 104: say happy: And more enemies. Lots more. You'll need them."` (the crowd overlay; M45a: + `"at 104: add crowd"`) · `"at 110: say happy: Grab the gems. You'll level up."` · `"at 126: say panic: That's a lot of them. That's the point!"`
  - `"at 143: path off"` · `"at 144: say thinking: Okay. Three games. I can only build one."`
- The Arsenal taste's card meter is 10 kills. The Auto taste forces a first level at 6 gems. **The Auto taste has no passives** (pistol + shotgun only), so it doesn't look like the strongest path just because it came last.
- debrief: `"thinking: So. Three versions of the same game."` · `"hit_lots worried: You took a beating in there. Prototypes, sorry."` · then `fork_path`. After the pick: the take line, then `"happy: The other two go in a drawer. I never throw anything away."`

**S13 · The checkpoint (`s2_07_boss`)**
- ***v9.2 (1.18):***
  - *You:* the Big Square, for real: rings of shots and a charge that leaves it dizzy. At half its health everything stops. The AI says it didn't do that. The screen tears and comes back as a turn-based fight: you on the left, the square on the right, your guns as a menu. Neither of you knows the rules, and you work them out together in a few turns. If you lose here, you start again from the moment it changed.

| `boss_hp_at_most 10` | "Almost! Almost!" | 3 | `s2_07_boss` |
| `player_hp_at_most 1` in the boss | "Careful! Checkpoint, remember?" | 3 | `s2_07_boss` |

| Session | Live changes (warned) | Mistake / gag |
|---|---|---|
| S7 | the whole game, twice (`path`) | "Saving that one.", as if a game were a file |
| S8 | adds the growth piece; paints you three times | "Everything's so grey." |
| S9 | repaints everything; the path edit; the walls go | the path edit goes slightly wrong (a shuffle, the farthest target, a rain of guns); the mascot surprised by its own colour |
| S10 | builds the parts; three new enemies; hot edges | "Level design is hard." |
| S11 | depth; scares the enemies | **depth**: everything falls at you; the crack |
| S12 | rebuilds the screen; polishes; builds a boss live | the boss does nothing and can't die; "I fixed the crack. Mostly." |
| S13 | the boss's edits (Patcher), hints, help, the AI fighting back | "First try?! I made it too easy." |

Every new rule or mechanic, with the scripted cue and line that adds it live, or why it is invisible. A test walks this table: each row's cue exists in that session's data for that path.

| Auto crowd overlay (×1.6 spawns, ×1.5 cap, ×0.7 HP) | S7 104 s (taste), S8 14 s | S7 `at 104` "And more enemies." · S8 `at 14` "I made the crowd bigger again." (M45a: + `add crowd`, the path basic that lands it) |
| Auto ×2 kill goal | S9 (the only kills goal) | S9 intro "Eighty kills for you." |

| Sidearm fallback at 0 ammo | S8 36 s | S8 `at 36` "Run dry and you get the pistol back." (the `ammo_empty` reaction is extra) |
| Kill-heal | S7 68 s, S8 5 kills | S7 `at 68` "Kills are food." · S8 `kills 5` |
| Battery (Survival orbit) | S11 part 1 16 s | "Yours run on a battery." |

## 4. Bot and debug
- **Policies** (`core/bot_input.gd`, chosen by `PathDef.bot_policy`):
  - **Arsenal:** picks offers by `pick_rng` (decorrelated from the fork picks), switches to the shotgun under 4 T, the laser when ≥ 3 enemies line up, the launcher into groups of 3+, else the best rank; **flanks a shielder** (steps 90° around it before firing).
  - **Auto:** **stops holding fire** (today's bot always holds it, which would never test auto-fire) and kites: away from the densest sector, toward gems when no enemy is within 3 T.
  - **Survival:** walks to the nearest pickup when no enemy is within 4 T, using **one multi-source BFS distance field over all pickups, recomputed at ~4 Hz** (per-pickup ExitGate fields at a cap of 60 are too slow); prefers a weapon it holds (rank up) or one with ammo; flanks shielders like Arsenal.
  - **Boss:** reads every attack's `DangerZone` (and the sniper's line, 1.9) and steps out of it, hits open cores first; otherwise the path policy.
- **What each policy can and cannot measure** (bot-runs-as-tuning-proxy; written next to every number it feeds):

  | Policy | Measures well | Cannot measure |
  |---|---|---|
  | Auto | pacing, level-up rate, crowd survival (move-only kiting; no aim skill involved) | whether picks matter (offers by seed) |
  | Arsenal | pacing, offer rate, that switching happens | whether card choice matters (`--probe=first_pick` is the only signal); quick-draw's value; `switches_per_min` is a policy constant |
  | Survival | pickup and dry-time rates, heal counts | mostly the policy's greed, not the design's: dry time and heals move with its 4 T rule |
  | Boss | ttk, phase length, dry time, telegraph timing (invincible) | difficulty: it dodges only what publishes a `DangerZone`, so mortal tries are a policy number |
  | all, mortal | time to death per path / enemy, with hit kinds | human difficulty. Reported, never gated; a miss means check the policy first |

- **Probes** (`--probe=<afk|no_pickup|no_switch|first_pick>`): degenerate strategies. **`afk` per path:** no move, and no fire on Arsenal / Survival; on Auto, afk still fires by design, so its bound is "≤ 50% of the default time to death, **or dies before the goal**". The others are reported (do pickups, switches and picks matter?).
- **Flags:** `--answer=<fork>=<change>` (repeatable) for every fork, with `--path=`, `--look=`, `--layout=`, `--hud=`, `--boss=` as short aliases; **`--loadout=<id:rank,…>`** (a fixed loadout for the whole session, no growth: the boss's DPS is measured with it, 1.10); `--help-level=<0-3>`; `--campaign` chains through S13; `--only=<session>` as in v8.
- **The campaign loop handles the new screens** (`_run_campaign` handles debrief forks only today; pick panels are handled only in the legacy loop, `bot_runs.gd:928`, and any death after attempt 1 picks "Move on", line 739):
  - **M45a** adds pick-panel handling to `_run_campaign` (with `pick_rng`).
  - **M49** adds the boss loop: at a checkpoint death, Retry (accepting help when offered) until it wins or 8 tries, then the line says `boss_unbeaten`. Each try is bounded on its own (`MAX_FRAMES` per try, not 45 min for all 8).
- **Fork coverage is forced, never left to `pick_seed`:** `_through_debrief` picks with `posmod(floor(pick_seed / div), n)`, so a chain lands on one path, and which one moves whenever an earlier fork changes. check.ps1's chains pass `--answer=` for every Stage 2 fork. **The coverage map** (written in check.ps1's header): chain A = Arsenal · Pixel · rooms · Clean · Square; chain B = Auto · Minimal · gauntlet · Arcade · Copycat; chain C = Survival · Splat · shifting · On you · Patcher. Every answer is played once by some chain.
- **Per-seed lines** add `path= weapons=pistol:3,laser:2 picks=n pickups=n heal=n level=n fp=…`, and for the boss `boss=<id> tries=n phase_deaths=[p1,p2,p3] ttk=s dry_max=s help=n`. `deaths:` adds `by_path=` and `by_boss_attack=`. Telemetry adds `enemy_shots_refused`.
- **`--mute-ai`** also mutes the pick panel's comments and the event's lines (the event still plays).
- **Perf bench** (`tools/bench.gd`): Auto at its enemy cap, 4 weapons + 2 passives at the top rank and 60 pickups, in each look. **Windowed, on the dev machine** (`godot --path . -s tools/bench.gd -- --look=<id>`): ≥ 60 fps; the export ≥ 45 fps at 1080p; the look pass ≤ 2 ms per frame at 1920×1080. These are dev-machine numbers, never in check.ps1.
- **`check.ps1`** (wall-time budget: **`-BotRuns` ≤ 15 min**, sharded over ~12 processes, per bot-runs-as-tuning-proxy):
  - **Rewritten in M43** (by id, not by index): `"index":7` → `"next":"s2_01_tryout"`, `chain: sessions=7` → the session count read from `campaign.tres`, `choice=to_be_continued` on s1_06 → on the last session, `v0.6.0->v0.7.0` → by session id, and export.ps1's `[campaign] 7 sessions: .*s1_06_playground; 30 reactions` → the counts read from data.
  - **Added in turn by the milestones:** chains A, B, C (invincible, S0–S13, sharded per stage), one mortal chain (report only), `--only` S7 (all three tastes play), S8 × 3 paths, S9 × 3 looks, S11 (the event plays fully), S13 × 3 bosses (each falls in the invincible run).
  - **Report-only, outside check.ps1:** `tools/stage2_sweep.ps1` runs the full per-path chains and the 16-seed tables (mortal deaths, boss tries, the `fp` tables) that the milestone reports quote.
- **`export.ps1`:** headless boots of S7, S8 × 3 paths, S9 and S13 × 3 bosses clean; the boot check wants "<N> sessions … <M> reactions" read from data. **Plus one windowed boot per look (tier 1 and tier 2)**, which runs `tools/look_check.gd` and checks its luminance and contrast numbers (shaders only compile with a real renderer).

## 5. Tests (additions)
- **Answers:** `Unlocks.answer/take` for every campaign fork (on `Fork.answer()`; every new change has a `MockupTake`); `fork:` resolution per field, `fork:fork_look@2` included; a `fork:` field before its fork is asked is a problem; `--answer=` equals the dash aliases byte for byte; a failing cue condition drops the cue at start; `&` joins any conditions, plain ones included; `!` negates; `attempts:n`, `help:n`, `died_in:` parse as one word; the `room N at s` grammar.
- **Pools:** per session × path, no pick, drop or crate carries a weapon before its `give` / tagged `say` cue; ranks stop at 3 until M50.1.
- **No-ops on Stage 1:** with `path = ""` every `WorldRules` field equals its M42.2 value; `Projectile` defaults leave the v6 hashes and the m42.2 campaign hash unchanged (M44); `hp_floor = 0` is a no-op.
- **In-run picks:** after 10 picks `RunState`'s stack and `build_number` are unchanged and nothing is written to the save or the patch notes.
- **Randomness:** S8 with drops on and off spawns the same enemy sequence for 60 s (`loot_rng` / `pick_rng` never touch `gameplay_rng`).
- **Budgets:** with Auto saturated, a sniper and a 16-shot boss ring still fire; `enemy_shots_refused` is logged; Stage 1 and the legacy loop keep the combined cap.
- **Weapons:** every `WeaponDef` is valid (ranks name real fields); each rank changes what it says; rank 4 adds its evolution, to that weapon's shots only; pellets × weapons at the caps stay under 200 projectiles; **with `path = ""` the pistol reads `Tuning` and fires byte-identically to M42.2** (both v6 hash compares and the Stage 1 chain hash match).
- **Paths:** each path's firing (Arsenal switches with quick-draw, Auto fires with the button up, Survival falls back to the sidearm at 0 ammo in one frame); growth fills and opens the panel; the panel waits for warnings and events; nothing persists past the session (the next session starts at pistol rank 1, level 1, no pickups, no overlay left); offers are seeded; path edits refuse on another path.
- **Pickups:** cap 60 with gem merging; lifetime and blink; drops by table; kill-heal capped at max HP; the dropped weapon is untouchable for 0.5 s.
- **Cues:** conditioned cues fire only on their answer; the glitch wipe flashes ≤ 3/s and is a crossfade under reduce flashing; `path` swaps and clears (no leftovers after each swap); `give` per path; `look` with seconds falls back; `room`, `boss_hp`, `level`, `picked` whens; `problems()` rejects a path basic under another path, a `path` cue outside a tryout and a `boss` goal without a boss.
- **Looks (v9.1):** each role and each id resolves in each look; the art data parses (grid sizes, legal letters); two bakes are byte-identical; no image files under game folders; silhouette mask IoU (1.7); `Look.path_for(1, take.id)` holds for the 3 new changes; the `LOOK_READERS` lint passes with `systems/palette.gd` and `systems/skins/skins.gd` added, no gameplay script reads `Palette` or `Skins`, and `systems/skins/` reads no gameplay RNG; **tier 0 matches `.check/ref_m47_2/tier0_*`** (windowed, same machine, ±1 LSB); `Look.try_on` mid-session reskins live nodes and restores (every skinned node at `Skins.version`); HUD and bubble text stay crisp; the luminance, contrast and distinctness checks (1.7, windowed); reduce flashing: no white flash, Splat's alpha capped.
- **Front door (v9.1):** pausing and resuming leaves every replay hash unchanged; `can_open()` refuses in each blocked case; Quit leaves no pause reason; settings parse additively (an M47 save loads; a bad new key falls back; a non-bool `reduce_flashing` still quarantines); routing raw vs title by progress; the debrief reads actions (AZERTY).
- **HUD:** each widget reads each style; the corner rule (M39.2) holds before `add hud`; no overlap with `Mascot.keep_out` / `bubble_rect` or the centre 70% (3 styles × 3 paths × 3 looks, v9.1).
- **Layouts:** each set plays its 3 parts in a session; `room N` cues; every exit reachable with every dash style × aim; `edit_hot_edges` never covers an exit tile.
- **Enemies:** `EnemyDB.BASES` is the only base list (the three copies are gone, `Unlocks.ARCHETYPES` is an alias); cues and sessions validate against `EnemyDB.all()`; the walker split keeps v6 hashes identical; each variant spawns, dies and splits as written; the shielder blocks front shots only, and blasts, pierce and orbit get through; the spatial hash runs only in path sessions.
- **Edits:** the reversibility invariant for each of the 6 (v8 5); `edit_screen_wrap` wraps the player, enemies and shots, and restores the walls.
- **Event:** `gravity_through_screen` plays its 6 steps in order; play is frozen (no damage, clock held, no spawns); **every body is back at its exact position and scale** (a state hash before = after, except enemies popped at 1 HP, which are logged); once per save; the gentle variant creates no white node; it aborts cleanly if the scene is freed; the crack persists to S12 and is a hairline after S12's polish.
- **Boss:** phases end on their `ends_on`; every attack telegraphs ≥ 0.6 s; dry time ≤ 8 s (logged per attempt); `max_seconds` moves to the next phase with its line and on P3 opens a damage window instead; **a win needs HP 0 or every part dead** (no timer, backstop or help level ends it otherwise); the Copycat reads the player's weapon, each dash style and the last pick; the Patcher's edits go through the rule book with warnings, and the mascot's revert works; **no Move on after a boss death** (attempts 1–8); Retry restarts the boss session; hints and help from the 2nd loss, each help level applies, help never ships; the dummy can't die and is removed.
- **Reactions:** the ~30 converted reactions fire in the same Stage 1 sessions as with `min_session`; the list of Stage 1 reactions that can fire in each `s2_*` session × path matches its fixture (`not_shooting` never on Auto); `when` gates (a gated reaction takes no cooldown slot); each new trigger fires on a scripted context.
- **Default-on audit:** every row of 3.5 has its cue in that session's data for that path.
- **Stage sets in sessions:** `prompts` is 0, in-run picks never count as prompts, a part's clear sweeps pickups, dropped weapons and crates; the v6 `stages` fork is never asked in a session.
- **Telemetry:** an M42.2 campaign line still parses; `CAMPAIGN_SCHEMA` is 4.
- **Save:** an M42.2 end-of-Stage-1 fixture continues at S7 with the stage card; an M42.2 save with index 7 maps to `next = s2_01_tryout`; inserting a session before the save's session leaves it pointing at the same id; `help` persists; nothing weapon-related is saved; schema stays 2.
- **Determinism:** the same seed plays **S7 (the three swaps)**, S8 (each path, the try-on included), **S11 (the event)** and S13 (each boss) twice with identical per-10-frame hashes, picks and pickups included.
- **Lines:** every intro ends 0.25 s before its first edit, event, `path` or `look` cue (M42.2's test, extended to every face × path × look × layout × HUD condition). The test reads the session data and the lines file, so a tone edit changes data only.
- **Legacy:** the v6 suite and both v6 hash compares pass unchanged at every milestone.

## 6. Milestones

Each milestone **ends playable**: the campaign plays from S0 to its last built session, with the new piece in it. "Bot" = a bot number; "human" = needs the owner; "**windowed**" = needs a real renderer on the dev machine (never in check.ps1; the report gives the command line). **Hard gates** are only: invincible pacing, determinism, hash compares, the telegraph / dry-time / backstop rules, "0 hits during the event" and tests. **Every mortal-bot number is reported with hit kinds, never pass/fail** (0.4); a mortal number far off its band means checking the bot policy first.

**Before M43 (on the `m42.2` tag, nothing changed yet):** record the m42.2 campaign hash (`run_hash.gd --campaign --session=<id>` for s1_03–s1_06, per second, entity kinds and positions plus kills) and the tier-0 screenshot reference (5 frames, windowed, same machine). Both live in `.check/ref_m42.2/` and are compared at M43, M43.1, M44, M45a, M45b and M48.

***v9.2 notes on the other rows:*** **M48.5** builds `UiTheme.use(look)` with Pixel's panel only (27 captures become 9). **M50** draws the shielder, sniper and swarm in Pixel only. **M52**'s tier 2 is Pixel's only (CRT, HUD digits, themed explosions). **M54**'s chains A / B / C are the three paths, all in Pixel.

`tools/check.ps1` is green before every commit. Each milestone gets a tag and a report, as before (reviews: 5 reviewers, 3 skeptics per finding). The report says which numbers are bot, which are windowed and which need a human, and quotes the path-only line counts.

**Why this order:** the answer plumbing first, because five forks ride on it and the dash's hand-wiring must not be copied five times. The enemy lists and the walker split alone next (M43.1), because a pure refactor is easiest to prove identical when nothing else moves. Then the weapon foundation, proven invisible on Stage 1 (identical hashes), because every path, the Copycat and the HUD widgets read it. Then the paths from cheapest to most new code (Auto reuses the most v6 code and brings `Pickup` with its gems, split into plumbing and scale; Arsenal adds the hand; Survival reuses `Pickup` and adds drops), so each is the previous one plus a small piece. **G14a comes before any content is stacked on the paths:** "do the three paths feel like three games?" is Phase 6's main question, and a wrong path is cheap to fix now and expensive once layouts, HUDs and bosses assume it. The looks next, because they are the other big question and every later screenshot uses them; the path edits land with S9, the only session that uses them, and the screen wrap gets its own step because it touches collision, the camera, the spawner and exits. **The boss slice comes right after (M49, G14b)**, because a sponge boss is the stage's biggest fun risk. Then content in play order. The evolutions wait for M50.1, the first point where a session is long enough to reach rank 4. The event needs the look (its proxies use the palette) and S11. The other bosses come last because they use everything. *v9.1:* the front door comes first (M47.3-M47.4) because the owner put it at the Stage 1 -> 2 door, and it is cheap and isolated (menus never step the simulation). M48 is split so that a "this looks amateur" verdict costs one revision of Pixel only (Splat reuses Pixel's grids). The UI styles follow the looks (M48.5), in the owner's order, because the skin dresses the panels; their story slot stays S11 / S12.

***v9.2:*** in the list below, the Splat, Minimal and Copycat items are already parked. New first cuts: the `flip` bug → the `tiles` bug → the torn patch-note line → Pull and Burst (Auto keeps Shot, Dodge and its top guns). **Never cut (v9.2):** the first-asset moment with at least one bug, the switch at 50% with the kit converted, Retry at the switch, the per-path boss tuning, one depth piece per path. "The three code-drawn looks with S8's try-on and S9's repaint" leaves the never-cut list.

**Cut order if needed (first to last; v9.1 items first):** Splat's live layer (the prebake stays) → Pixel's HUD digits → Minimal's tier-2 shatter → window scale and Mute when unfocused → the pixel font (the fallback font) → pause's Restart → the 3 s path clips (stills stay) → the pick panel's comments → the Copycat's phase 3 (one copy) → `edit_shuffle_hand` / `edit_auto_wrong` / `edit_weapon_rain` (S9 keeps `edit_screen_wrap` only) → reactions down to 10 → rank-4 evolutions (ranks stop at 3; ranks 2–3 keep their behaviours) → `fork_hud` (kept by the owner, v9.1; cut only here: the AI picks from your `fp` and announces it in S12) → the orbit (S11 brings the enemies and the event) → S12 merged into S13's intro (the dummy becomes the boss's first 20 s). **Never cut:** the three paths on one foundation, the three code-drawn looks with S8's try-on and S9's repaint, the title, options and pause (v9.1), S7's playable tastes, the three S10 enemies and their meeting, the checkpoint rule (no Move on, unlimited retries, hints and help, a win only at HP 0), the anti-sponge rules, gravity through the screen, the default-on audit (3.5), "power stays in the session" (unless G14a says otherwise), G14a and G14.

### 6.1 As built (M43): answers and the Stage 2 door
Step 0 (PLAN v8 archived as `PLAN.v8.md`, v9 installed) was commit 05c069d. Before any code changed, the m42.2 references were recorded on the tree that equals tag `m42.2` in code, in `.check/ref_m42.2/`: the campaign hash (`tools/run_hash.gd --campaign`, new: s1_03-s1_06 x seeds 1-4, one md5 per game second of player position, every live entity's kind and position, kills, build number and stack; the tool answers every Stage 1 fork from the seed itself, so how a jump answers forks cannot move it; 16 runs, a second recording compared 16/16 the same), the Stage 1 invincible chain (`play=290.0`, picks `mascot_blob,dash_grapple,dashdir_aim` then `enemy_tank`, the stripped lines) and the `--only=s1_04_level --dash=grapple 3i` line. **The tier-0 screenshot reference was not recorded** (it needs a windowed renderer on the dev machine; this step ran headless only), so M43's screenshot compare is skipped; M43 changes no Stage 1 rendering (the only new visual, the stage card, shows from Stage 2 on). Record it windowed with `tools/screenshot.gd` before M44 touches the projectile visuals.
- **Answers (1.1):**
  - `Unlocks.answer(fork_id)` (the shipped option, or null) and `Unlocks.take(fork_id)` (its take id, or `&""`; the enemy changes have no take). `dash_answer()` / `dash_aim_answer()` stand on `take()` (fallbacks classic / move); `SessionDef.enemy_types()` and the runner's `spawn fork:` resolution stand on `answer()`. `ARCHETYPES` is untouched (M43.1).
  - `SessionDef.fork_of(value)` (the `<id>` of `fork:<id>[@n]`), `resolve(field)` (a `fork:` field's take), `fork_refs()` (every fork the session names: enemies, `spawn`/`look`/`boss` cues, path, look, stages, hud, boss). `CampaignDB.problems()`: every `fork_refs()` id must be asked by an **earlier** session.
  - `CampaignDB.answer_fork(fork, change)`: validates the option, ships it with **PATCH** if the fork is unanswered, and a mascot option sets the body. `answer_dash` / `answer_dash_aim` delegate to it (keeping their own warnings). `answer_forks(s)` stays (the session's own `fork:` enemies) and `answer_earlier_forks(id)` adds option 0 for every unanswered fork asked before `id`.
  - `CampaignDB.answers_from_args(args)`: every `--answer=<fork>=<change>`, then the aliases `--dash=<s>` / `--dash-aim=<a>` (`none` answers nothing). One rule for the game and the bot: an `--answer=` wins over its alias for the same fork wherever it stands, and among valid flags of one kind the first wins (a first `--dash=none` decides too). The game reads them **only inside the `--session=` branch** (`CampaignDB.read_jump_args(args)`, which `session_for_game()` calls with the real command line), then answers earlier forks with option 0 (D3).
  - `CampaignDB.shelf(stage)`: every shelved option of that stage's forks (`Fork.shelf()`), tested on Stage 1.
  - Bot: a repeatable, validated `--answer=`; after its arg loop the bot builds `answers` with the game's own `CampaignDB.answers_from_args()`, so the same flags answer the same forks in a bot line and a game jump. `--dash=` keeps its first value (the `none` lock and the printed dash), and `--dash=none` together with an `--answer=` for `fork_dash` fails. `--only` ships them before the seed answers its enemy fork (`_answer_forks` keeps an already answered fork); the chain's debrief picks a named option for any fork. `--only` keeps its own answering (no option 0 for earlier forks).
- **Line conditions (1.1):** `&` joins any conditions (`JOINABLE` is gone), `!` negates one (and still fails when the context lacks its key, `Line.KEYS`), `attempts:<n>` (attempt >= n, n >= 1) and `died_in:<session>` (reads `campaign.attempts`; `line_context()` adds `attempts_by_session`). `Line.condition_problem()` / `condition_holds()` / `condition_parts()` are shared by lines, cues and reactions; the empty condition holds. `died_in:` ids are checked against the campaign (lines, cue conditions, reaction lines and `when`). `help:n` is M49.
- **The cue grammar (1.3):** `[cond] <when> [N] [at s] [[tag]]: <action>`. The head ends at the first word ending with `:` (so `answer:path_auto at 12:` parses; every Stage 1 cue parses as before, tested). New whens `room N [at s]`, `boss_hp N` (0-100), `level N`, `picked N` (never due in M43: nothing supplies their keys); new actions `path`, `give [rank 1-4]`, `drop [n]`, `look [seconds]`, `boss [dummy]`, syntax-checked. A cue whose condition fails is dropped in `SessionRunner._ready` before `_fired` is sized. `SessionDef.problems()`: `path` cues need `tryout`, `room` needs a stage set, `boss_hp` needs a boss. **`CampaignDB.problems()` refuses in data what has no runtime yet ("not built yet", D4):** the five new actions, the four new whens, a `[tag]` cue (`Cue.UNBUILT_*`), and the fields below.
- **SessionDef fields (1.2):** `path`, `weapons`, `stages`, `hud`, `boss`, `checkpoint`, `tryout`, `spawn_weights`, `hp_floor`; goals `stages` / `boss` (stubs: never end on the clock, `RunLoop` passes). Shape checks: path in `PATHS` or `fork:`, a stage set file or `fork:`, goal stages <=> a set, goal boss needs a boss and checkpoint, a boss needs goal boss, hud only `fork:`, `spawn_weights` keys in `ARCHETYPES` with weights > 0, `hp_floor >= 0`. "Not built yet": weapons (M44), path (M45a), stages / goal stages and spawn_weights (M50), hud (M51), boss / goal boss and checkpoint (M49). `look` takes `fork:<id>` and `@tier`; `look_spec()` gives `{tier, take}` (take a String; an unanswered fork is tier 0) and `Unlocks.look()` uses it; the preset check is `look_<tier>_<take>.tres`, for each option of a fork.
- **The HP floor:** `WorldRules.hp_floor` (0 = exact no-op), set as base from the session in `preview_rules()`. `main.gd` recomputes right after `RunState.session` is set, only when the floor is > 0 (so Stage 1 sees no extra `rules_changed`). `Health.take()` for the player: `max(hp - amount, min(hp, floor))`, so a hit at the floor leaves 1 HP; the hit still counts, signals and grants i-frames.
- **Reactions (1.13):** `min_session` / `max_session` became `after` / `until` (session ids, inclusive) and `when` (a Line condition, checked when the session starts, so a gated reaction is never loaded and takes no cooldown slot). `ReactionDef.fits(id)`; `Reactions.session_id` (main.gd). The 30 files converted 1:1 (3 = s1_03_shooting ... 6 = s1_06_playground; tested against the frozen old table over the 7 Stage 1 sessions). **The audit:** `when = "!answer:path_auto"` on `not_shooting`, `crowd_12`, `kills_50`, `streak_3`, `streak_4`, `streak_6`, `streak_broken` (7 of 30); the other 23 are unchanged. `tests/gut/stage2_reactions.json` lists the reactions that can fire in `s2_01_tryout` x (none, arsenal, auto, survival): 25 / 25 / 18 / 25. The `answer:` guard test now covers reaction `when`s and cue conditions, with `PLANNED_CHANGES` (`path_arsenal`, `path_auto`, `path_survival`; emptied at M47).
- **Save by id (1.14), schema still 2:** `campaign.next` (an id; `""` = after the furthest done). `_parse_campaign` keeps `next` only when the file has it (a wrong type is corrupt). `campaign_done(..., index_after, next_after)` and `set_campaign_position(index, next)`; `index` stays as the derived mirror. `CampaignDB.current_index()` is a pure function of the save: a known `next`; else, for a file without the key, its `index` through the frozen `M42_2_ORDER` (7 or more = the session after s1_06, `s2_01_tryout`; "index 3, done [s1_00]" stays s1_03); else the session after the furthest done (D1). `complete()` and `jump_to()` write both.
- **The stage card (2):** `CampaignDef.stage_titles` (`1: "The prototype"`, `2: "The shooter"`; `problems()` wants one per stage a session names). `ui/stage_card.gd` (`StageCard`, a CanvasLayer, `PROCESS_MODE_ALWAYS`): black, "Stage 2", the title typed at `Mascot.TYPE_CPS`, a `Mascot` with the save's body under it saying "Okay. Now we make it a real game."; 3 s on frame time; a key after 0.5 s closes it; `skip()` always does. It pauses through `RunState.set_pause(&"stage_card")`, writes no telemetry and bumps nothing. `CampaignDB.stage_card_due(s)`: stage > 1, titled, and no session of that stage done or retried. The bot and `run_hash.gd` skip it on the first frame; the chain line then ends with `card=<stage>` (after `version=`). **The mascot was built** (no text-only fallback).
- **S7 as a stub** (`campaign/sessions/s2_01_tryout.tres`): stage 2, "Three games", S6's 8 basics and enemies and spawn 1.3 / 1.3, survive 60 s, `hp_floor = 1`, `tryout`, face, a minor bump (v0.7.0 -> v0.8.0), my stub lines (intro 3, one cue at 30 s, one `ended` debrief line), notes "The shooter, three ways (work in progress)", no fork. S6 now offers Next session; S7 offers To be continued. `BUILD = "M43"`.

- **Tests:** new `test_answers.gd` (19 after the review) and `test_stage2_door.gd` (17); updated `test_campaign.gd` (8 sessions; S6 Next, the data's last session To be continued; v0.8.0; `next` in a new campaign; `campaign_done` with the next id), `test_dash_aim.gd` (the join test now positive for plain conditions; the extended `answer:` guard), `test_reactions.gd` (`fits()` and `reaction_problems()`), `test_save.gd` (`next` parsed only when present). `test_events.gd` is unchanged (`answer_forks` kept).

### 6.2 As built (M43.1): enemy lists + the walker split
A pure refactor: no behaviour, spawn logic, data or tool changed. The one visible change is `BUILD = "M43.1"` (the start screen's "prototype build" line and the telemetry `build` field).
- **The tier-0 screenshot reference (the one M43 skipped)** was recorded first, windowed, on the clean tree at tag `m43` (b10c552), not on `m42.2`: 6.1 says M43 changed no Stage 1 rendering. `.check/ref_m42.2/capture_tier0.gd` (not a repo tool) goes inert, jumps to `s1_06_playground` (earlier forks answered with option 0), seeds the run (`Main.next_seed`), asserts `Look.label() == "0:-"`, then does `tools/screenshot.gd`'s body (invincible bot, director off, `spawn_wave(10)`, 60 + 70 physics frames). Frames `tier0_1..5.png` (seeds 1-5, 1280x720), OpenGL 3.3 Compatibility on an AMD Radeon RX 7900 XTX, Godot 4.7.stable; details in `tier0_info.txt`. `tools/screenshot.gd` itself cannot capture tier 0 (no session, so `Unlocks.look()` is look_1_punchy).
  - Self-diff (a second run on m43): seeds 3 and 4 identical; seed 1 max delta 195 on 109 px, seed 2 232 on 1 px, seed 5 81 on 20 px (lit telegraphs: `rect_visual.gd:71` blinks on the wall clock). So it is a **visual reference only, not a ±1 LSB baseline**. For M48's "±1 LSB on 5 frames" to be measurable, M44 should either capture at a frame with no telegraph lit or pin the blink clock (a gameplay-file change, so M44's call).
  - After the refactor (`tier0_post`, idle machine): seeds 2, 3, 4 identical; seed 1 216 on 198 px, seed 5 134 on 22 px (the same blink class). A first post run made while the full GUT suite ran in parallel diverged on seeds 3-5 (different game states, ~250k px). The idle rerun matched, and the headless hashes match, so the capture is sensitive to load (frame-time reads: the session runner's line queue and the first frames under `HumanInput` before the bot takes over are candidates). Capture on an idle machine.
- **`EnemyDB`** (`enemies/enemy_db.gd`, `class_name EnemyDB extends RefCounted`): `BASES` (the five ids, in the legacy order), `VARIANTS` (empty until M47), `all()` (a fresh typed `BASES` + `VARIANTS`), `has(id)` (no allocation), `scene_path(id)` (`res://entities/enemy_<id>.tscn`, bases only) and `base_scenes()`. No preloads, so no load cycle through Unlocks.
- **The three lists became one:** `Unlocks.ARCHETYPES` is `EnemyDB.BASES` (an alias); `test_save.gd:315` is byte-identical. `Cue.ARCHETYPES` is gone: the `spawn` check reads `EnemyDB.has()` (1.3 cites `cue.gd:29`; since M43 it was at :44). `SessionDef.problems()` checks `enemies` and `spawn_weights` keys with `EnemyDB.has()` (the same as `all()`). `Spawner.SCENES` is now a `static var` from `EnemyDB.base_scenes()` (same name for `topdown_genre.gd` and the tests; the same cached PackedScenes; no static-init, cyclic or load error in the import, boot or GUT logs, so the fallback of keeping const preloads was not needed).
- Left on the alias on purpose: `ChangeDB`'s unlock check (1.3), and the fork-answer resolvers `session_def.gd:132` / `session_runner.gd:132` (ChangeDB limits unlocks to bases; M47 decides whether a variant can be a fork unlock, and then all three move together). `Spawner.pick_type()` keeps its literal names (spawn logic; M50's `spawn_weights` replace it in path sessions).
- **The walker split (inheritance with hooks):** `entities/enemy_walker.gd` is now `class_name EnemyWalker`, the shared walk only (403 -> 207 lines), with four hooks whose defaults keep the chaser and tank as before: `_before_tick(delta)` (true skips the frame), `_attack_step(delta, to_player, distance)` (true ends the frame), `_shows_rim()` and `_on_died(info)`. `_physics_process` keeps its statement order, and the one `randi()` in `_ready` stays in the base. `entities/enemy_bomber.gd` (120 lines: the fuse, the blast, `blast_radius()`, the campaign death fuse) and `entities/enemy_lancer.gd` (100 lines: `Lance`, the charge, the line) extend it, with their bodies copied verbatim. `enemy_bomber.tscn` and `enemy_lancer.tscn` name their own scripts; the chaser and tank scenes keep the base (no empty subclasses). Scene paths are unchanged (`reactions.gd` and `enemies_split` read them). `is_bomber()` / `is_lancer()` are gone (nothing outside read them).
- **Tests:** new `test_enemy_db.gd` (6: the lists and `all()`'s type; the alias; `Spawner.SCENES` from BASES; a one-list lint (the five-name and any-3-in-a-row patterns only in `enemy_db.gd`, no second dictionary of enemy-scene preloads, no `Cue.ARCHETYPES`); cues and sessions read `all()` (exact problem texts); the split's shape (scripts, base script, no bomber/lancer/fuse/Lance code in the base, `archetype` and `contact_reach()` on each)). `test_bomber.gd` preloads `enemy_bomber.gd` for `blast_radius()` (two calls); no other existing test changed.

The beat, warning (1.5 s), grace per flip, spawn pause, clock hold and +1 HP sorry heal are unchanged; the record is `@20.0-39.0` (was `@40.0-51.5`), the clock and spawns held 17.5 s (was 10).

While held, `_update_goal()` keeps the clock but ends nothing (kills, time limit, survive, stop); a kills goal reached while held logs `goal_waited` once and wins on the first tick after the release (after "Speed was the other slider." and the heal). A death still ends the session at once. The HUD already clamped the shown kills (`Kills 25/25`, dimmed while the clock is held). The legacy loop (`goal == ""`) never reaches this code.

### 6.4 As built (M44): weapons, ranks 1-3
Weapons exist and are proven invisible on Stage 1: with no path the player holds the pistol at rank 1, which fires through the pre-M44 `Shooter.try_fire` call. Every new `Projectile` field defaults to today's behaviour. Both v6 hashes, the m43.3 campaign hash and every Stage 1 `WorldRules` field are unchanged. `BUILD = "M44"`.
- **References first (before any code, on tag m43.3, tree clean), `.check/ref_m43.3/`:** the campaign hash `campaign_hash.jsonl` (s1_03-s1_06 x seeds 1-4: s1_03 25/25/23/25 s, s1_04 31 s x4, s1_05 62/63/62/58 s with 20/21/20/20 kills, s1_06 54/56/52/52 s with 40/41/41/41 kills). Self-compare **16/16, 0 failures**. Its four S4 lines equal `ref_m42.2`'s byte for byte, and s1_04 vs m42.2 gave **4/0**. The v6 "before" logs also live here (4/0 and 4/0).
  - The Stage 1 rules fixture is `tests/gut/rules_stage1_m43_3.json`. It was recorded by `.check/ref_m43.3/dump_rules.gd` through the shared helper `tests/gut/rules_dump.gd`, so the gate test cannot drift from it. The procedure: an inert save, every Stage 1 fork answered from seed 1 as `run_hash.gd` does, `jump_to`, seed 1, the card skipped, 2 physics frames. It holds every script field of `rules` and `base_rules` for s1_00..s1_06 plus a plain `reset(1)`, and every session has `path == ""`.
  - The same dump on an `m42.2` worktree (`rules_m42_2.json`, worktree removed after) differs **only by `hp_floor`** (16 entries: 7 sessions plus legacy, x rules/base_rules; the field did not exist at m42.2). So the gate's baseline is m43.3, checked once against m42.2.
  - A windowed tier-0 capture on an m43.3 worktree (`tier0_1..5.png`) is the "pre" picture for Step 8.
- **Data (`weapons/`, export-safe, no preloads):**
  - `WeaponDef` (1.4's fields; `ranks[0]` = rank 2 and `ranks[1]` = rank 3, absolute values; `traits` is the full cumulative list). Its methods: `value(field, rank)`, `traits_at`, `max_rank()` (3), `is_passive()` and `problems()`. `problems()` checks the id against the file name, the mode, the cooldown and pellets, that rank keys are real fields, that every trait exists (`ResourceLoader.exists`), that traits are cumulative, "rank 4 is not built yet (M50.1)", and one card line per rank.
  - `WeaponDB` (static, RuleDB pattern: `all/ids/get_weapon/has/reload/problems`).
  - Five resources (titles, pitches and card lines are my drafts; the lines file comes at M47): `pistol` (0.18 / 900 / 1), `shotgun` (5 pellets, 28 deg, 0.55 s, 0.35 s life, ammo 24; rank 3 also `spread_deg 18`), `laser` (1600, pierce 3, dmg 2, 0.6 s, size 1.5, ammo 16), `launcher` (600, blast 1.6 T, dmg 2, 0.9 s, size 1.6, ammo 10), `orbit` (passive, `pellets` = 2 shards, dmg 1, 0.4 s per enemy). Rank 2 / rank 3 traits: pistol `heavy_round` / `+seeker`; shotgun `knockback_cone` / `+point_blank`; laser `burn_line` / `+ricochet`; launcher `sticky` / `+implode`; orbit `flare` / `+guard`.
  - `Weapon` (runtime, never saved): rank, `ready_in`, `shots`, `ammo_left`, traits rebuilt on a rank change, `label()` ("Shotgun II"). **D3 lives in `Weapon.value()`:** the pistol with no path reads `Tuning.shoot_cooldown` / `projectile_speed` at every rank.
- **Traits (`weapons/traits/`, all deterministic, no RNG, plain colours, no `Look`):** `WeaponTrait` hooks `shot / spawned / passive_ready / dashed`. Projectile-side behaviour is a `ProjectileMod` child (`steer / hit_entity / hit_wall / despawned`).
  - `heavy_round`: every 5th shot `(shots + 1) % 5`: x3 damage, x2 size, pierce 1.
  - `seeker`: turns toward the nearest enemy within 4 T, at most 60 deg/s, re-picked each tick.
  - `knockback_cone`: knockback impulse 420, which moves a chaser 0.5 T +- 0.15 in GUT.
  - `point_blank`: x2 damage on a hit within 2 T of the muzzle.
  - `burn_line`: `burn_line_zone.gd` along the path the shot flew: a polyline from the muzzle through each wall contact (bounce) and each turn (gravity, collinear ticks merged) to where it ended (M44 review; never the straight chord). It lasts 1 s and deals 1 damage per 0.5 s to each enemy within its half width + 4 px. Its kind is `burn`, credited to `weapon:laser`.
  - `ricochet`: `bounces_left 1`, and that bounce resets pierce and the skip list.
  - `sticky`: stops on the first contact, follows the enemy, and blasts 0.6 s (game time) later at x1.2 the radius.
  - `implode`: an inward 455 impulse 0.15 s before the blast.
  - `flare`: `player_dashed` doubles the shard radius for 0.6 s.
  - `guard`: a shard eats an `enemy_projectile` within 7 + 4 px and is gone 1 s.
  - **No trait fell back to a number.**
- **Projectile (`entities/projectile.gd`), every default = today's behaviour:**
  - The new fields: `pierce_left` + `_pierced` (merged into each ray's `exclude`; empty at pierce 0), `bounces_left` (used only after `rules.projectile_max_bounces` is spent: `elif` after the rules' branch), `size` (visual only, D21), `lifetime` (-1 = Tuning), `weapon_id`, `blast_radius` (px; contact -> `Explosion.blast(.., 0 knockback, &"blast", source, push_player=false)`), `knockback` (-1 = Tuning), `origin`, `_mods`, `_held`.
  - `_hit_entity` returns "keep going". With pierce 0, no blast and no mods, the statement order is exactly the old one.
  - `steer` runs before the gravity step. Mods take wall and entity contacts first.
  - The new public methods are `add_mod / mods / hold / despawn / clear_pierced / will_weapon_bounce`.
  - **Kill credit (D2):** weapon shots carry `source_patch = "weapon:<id>"`. Legacy pistol shots carry nothing, so they are still credited to `base`. `ReportScreen._source_title` maps `weapon:<id>` to the weapon's title.
- **`Shooter.fire(weapon, dir, auto)`:** the same muzzle check as before. It fires an even fan `-spread/2 + spread * i / (n-1)` (no RNG), with each trait's `shot()` before `RunState.spawn` (a pellet refused at the cap is skipped) and `spawned()` after. It emits `projectile_fired` per pellet (D11) and never touches `_ready_in`. `try_fire` is byte-identical.
- **Orbit (D15, D19):** `mutations/orbit/orbit_shards.gd` keeps its constants and gains `shard_count / radius / damage / hit_cooldown` vars (same values), `shard_active(i)` (true) and `damage_source()` (`source_patch`). `weapons/orbit_weapon_shards.gd` extends it: radius 2 T, flare, guard, credited to `weapon:orbit`, with an empty `Component.source_patch` (never in `snapshot()`). G1/G2 were run right after this edit.
- **`Loadout` (`components/loadout.gd`, last child of `player.tscn`, scene LF, `load_steps` 9):** `slots` (aimed), `passives`, `active`, `fire_mode = manual_one`, `max_slots = 3`, `MAX_RANK = 3`.
  - `legacy_pistol()` is true with no path and the pistol at rank 1. `try_fire` then makes today's call. Otherwise the weapon's own `ready_in` sets its cooldown and `shots` counts after `fire()` returns.
  - `give(id, rank)`: a held weapon goes to `max(cur + 1, rank)`, capped (false at 3). A new aimed weapon takes a free slot; with a full hand it replaces the weakest non-active slot (lowest rank, then oldest). A passive attaches its node. There is no auto-switch.
  - `set_weapon` (exact rank: bot, bench, tests), `upgrade`, `switch(step)` (aimed only), `select`, `clear()` (back to the pistol at rank 1), `summary()` (`pistol:1,shotgun:2`) and `rank_of`. Each change emits `EventBus.weapon_changed(id, rank)` (1.6).
  - `player.gd` routes `try_fire`, `can_fire` and `auto_fire()` through the Loadout, and calls `loadout.switch(input.switch_step)` after `shooting_locked`, before fire. B2's early return and `turn_clock.gd` are untouched.
- **Input (D17):** `weapon_prev` = Q / wheel up / LB and `weapon_next` = E / wheel down / RB (E is still `wait`). `PlayerInput.switch_step` is set for one tick. `HumanInput` reads Q/E/bumpers with `is_action_just_pressed`. The wheel goes through `_input` into a pending step, consumed on the next physics tick and cleared under `_wait_release`. `BotInput` leaves it at 0. Q and E are physical positions (like WASD): on AZERTY the Q position is printed A, so a line names keys with `{key:<action>}` tokens, which `SessionRunner.say` expands through `InputSetup.key_label` (moved there from `InvertedHint`, which now delegates) and `Line.parse` checks (`InputSetup.keys_problem`: known action, closed token).
- **Randomness (D13):** `RunState.loot_rng` / `pick_rng`, seeded in `reset()` with `hash([seed, "loot" / "pick"])`. Nothing draws from them yet, and nothing in `weapons/` draws from any stream.
- **Campaign:** `give` left `Cue.UNBUILT_ACTIONS`. `UNBUILT_TAG`'s comment now says "the pool rule comes with offers (M45a)" (the 1.3 drift is fixed; the pool rule stays M45a, D6).
  - `CampaignDB._unbuilt_problems`: the "weapons are not built yet" line is gone, replaced by "rank 4 is not built yet (M50.1)" for a `give` above `Loadout.MAX_RANK`.
  - `SessionDef._weapon_problems`: known ids, no duplicates, a non-empty `weapons` lists `pistol`, and every `give` target is in `weapons` ("give x: not in the session's weapons").
  - `SessionRunner` `give`: `Loadout.give(target, rank)` on the genre's player, with a warning when refused. With no path, `give` means "a free slot" (paths redefine it, M45a-M47).

---

### 6.5 As built (M45a): path plumbing + the Auto taste
Paths exist, and Auto is playable as a taste (S7 stub) and as a draft (S8). Stage 1 and the legacy loop do not move: no PathRunner, no pick panel, no listener and no node is added to a session without a path or a tryout (D11). Both v6 hashes, the m43.3 campaign hash and every Stage 1 `WorldRules` field are unchanged. `BUILD = "M45a"`.
- **References first (`.check/ref_m45a/`):** the "before" S7 stub lines on the M44 tree (`before_s7_3i.log`, `before_s7_3.log`: `outcome=win t=60.0 ... weapons=pistol:1,shotgun:1`). G3 compares to `ref_m43.3`, G4 to `ref_m42.2`. No new recording.
- **Data (shared, not path-only):**
  - `paths/path_def.gd` (`PathDef`, 1.5's fields plus `max_passives`, `basics`, `level_thresholds`, `taste_thresholds`, `max_picks`, `warn_line`). `threshold(level, tryout)` reads the taste list in a tryout (-1 past it: the meter stops quietly) or the level list, then +20 a level. `problems(file_id)`.
  - `paths/path_db.gd` (`PathDB`, static, the RuleDB pattern; `owner_of_basic()`), scanning `res://paths/*.tres`.
  - `paths/auto.tres`: `auto_all`, `levelups`, start `[pistol]`, 4 slots, 2 passives, `offer_pool_extra = []` (D3), ×1.6 / ×1.5 / ×0.7, kills ×2.0, boss ×1.3, widget `level`, bot policy `auto`, basics `[levelups, crowd]`, levels 3, 8, 15, 24, 36, 50, 66, 84 (+20), taste `[6]` (D5), 10 picks, warn line "worried: New version. Hands off the trigger." Only Auto exists (D4).
- **Runtime:**
  - `RunState.session_path` (S7's cue and F2; `reset()` clears it), `live_path()` (`&"off"` = none) and `path_def()`. `Weapon._no_path()` reads `live_path()`, the one shared-path change.
  - Pickups get their own cap (`Tuning.hard_max_pickups = 60`) and `pending_count(kind)`. New EventBus signals: `pickup_taken`, `pick_offered`, `pick_made`, `level_up`. Pause reason `&"pick"`.
  - **The crowd overlay** (`PathOverlay`, a session-layer `Mutation`, id `path_auto_crowd`, owner `&"path"`) lands with the new campaign basic **`crowd`** (D2), Auto's second path basic. It stays until the next scene's `RunState.reset()`: expiring it at the run's end would bump the build and log under the debrief.
  - **Auto fire:** `Loadout.apply_path(def)` resets the loadout to the path's mode, slots and start weapons at exactly rank 1. `Loadout.auto_tick()` fires each aimed slot off its cooldown at `AutoFire.target_for` (the nearest visible enemy within 12 T; a blast weapon aims at the densest group). The player calls it instead of the fire button on `auto_all`. `player._auto_target` is untouched (D9).
  - **Pickup** (`entities/pickup.gd/.tscn`, shared): gems join the `gem` group and never expire. Gems are pulled from 3 T and fly at 600 px/s; other kinds are pulled from 0.6 T, live 14 s and blink for their last 3 s. A pickup is taken within 0.5 T, and only by a living player (D10).
  - **Auto's gems** (`paths/auto/gems.gd`) drop on player kills only (tank and bomber 3, the rest 1). At the cap, the oldest gem merges into its nearest gem. This draws nothing (lowest serial, nearest by distance), so the merge needs no `loot_rng`. When every gem is still entering the tree in that frame, the newest gem takes the value, so no gem's worth is lost.
  - **The level meter** (`paths/auto/level_meter.gd`) stops at 10 picks with "thinking: That's enough levels for one test." (D6). A tryout meter stops quietly after one level. The widget (`paths/auto/level_widget.gd`) shows `LV n`, the held weapons and a bar, under the corner text (`hud.set_path_widget`).
  - **`PickOffer`** draws up to 3 cards from `pick_rng` out of the live pool ∩ (session weapons + `offer_pool_extra`). It uses the session-local `Taste` raw weights, ×1.4 on upgrades (D7). A `give` cue forces card 1. Ranks stop at `Loadout.MAX_RANK` 3 (and passives at `max_passives`, aimed weapons at `max_slots`), and the filler is "+1 HP (this session)".
  - **`PickPanel`** is its own CanvasLayer (layer 72), **not a PromptPanel subclass** (deviation from 0.4 / 1.5's wording; PromptPanel is untouched, so G1/G2 cannot move through it). It pauses through `&"pick"`, uses keys `choose_1..3` with `{key:choose_n}` labels, and has the 0.4 s real-time input delay. `dismiss()` always drops the pause reason.
  - **`PathRunner`** (`paths/path_runner.gd`, shared) handles the following:
    - Start or swap. A `path` cue says the target's `warn_line` at priority 3, then swaps 1.5 s later on the physics tick clock. A swap clears player shots and pickups, keeps enemies and plays one `Transition.glitch(0.6)` pulse, or a 0.4 s fade under reduce flashing (D12).
    - The growth and the crowd, each as its basic arrives.
    - **The live pool:** the start weapons, then each `give` or `say [tag]` cue. The pool is kept on the runner across swaps, not on SessionRunner (deviation from the map).
    - The picks. A panel never opens over a rule edit's warning, an event, a pending swap, another pause or the end of the run. A live timed edit does not hold picks, so this is not `RuleBook.busy()`.
    - The mascot's word after a pick (priority 1, muted with `--mute-ai`; D8).
    - `stop()` on `run_ended`. Nothing is saved.
  - `SessionRunner` handles `path` cues and a `give` under a levelups path: the weapon is offered first at the next level-up, and no slot is filled. Its context gains `level` and `picks`, which drive the `level N` and `picked N` whens.
  - `TopdownGenre` multiplies a **kills** goal by `kill_goal_mult`; survive goals never scale. `Reactions` reads the run loop's goal for the kills progress (`_kill_goal()`). `_survive_left` still reads `session.goal_value`, because `--lift` sets the run loop's value and check.ps1's `bot_edits` uses `--lift=30`. `not_shooting` is silent on `auto_all`.
  - The F2 debug overlay gains a PATH row: off / auto / arsenal (M46, disabled) / survival (M47, disabled), plus "+1 level". F1 gains `path=<id> level=n picks=n pickups=n`.
- **Data checks:**
  - `Cue.UNBUILT_*` lose `path`, `give`'s `[tag]`, `level` and `picked`. A plain `path` that is not in PathDB is refused as "path arsenal is not built yet (M46)" or "path survival is not built yet (M47)". `path off` passes.
  - A `[tag]` must be in the session's weapons or a path's `offer_pool_extra`.
  - Path basics follow 1.2's rule as built.
  - The carry rule (CampaignDB) skips path basics.
  - New campaign basics: `levelups` and `crowd`.
- **Content:**
  - **The S7 stub** (`s2_01_tryout`) is an Auto taste. Its cues:
    - `at 13: path auto`
    - `at 16` "It shoots for you."
    - `at 20: add crowd` + "And more enemies."
    - `at 24: give shotgun` + "I put a shotgun in your next level-up."
    - `at 28: add levelups` + "Grab the gems."
    - `picked 1` "See? Level up."
    - `at 47` "That's a lot of them."

    **Deviation:** the map's `at 10` path cue would land inside the 11.76 s intro (`Reactions.speak_seconds`, which the dash-aim spacing test uses), so every cue moved after it.
  - **The S8 draft** (`campaign/drafts/s8_draft.tres`, D1) is outside `campaign.tres`. It plays path `auto`, survives 120 s, has `hp_floor 0` and uses the S6 basics. Its cues: `at 10: add levelups`, `at 14: add crowd`, `at 30: give shotgun`, each with its line. M47 turns it into `s2_02_day_one`.
- **Bot and tools:**
  - **BotInput** reads `PathDef.bot_policy` every tick; `""` is today's code, byte for byte. The `auto` policy never holds fire. It kites away from the densest of 8 sectors within 6 T when an enemy is within `keep_distance`, else walks to the nearest gem when no enemy is within 3 T, else orbits; an open exit gate always wins, also with no enemy left (gate, then gem, then orbit; review fix). `--probe=afk` gives no move, no dash, no jump and no fire.
  - **`bot_runs.gd`** gains `--draft=<id>` (inserted at `cooking_index()`) and `--probe=afk`. Picks are chosen from `RunState.pick_rng`. With a path, `--loadout=` is forced after each path start and growth freezes (D13). The `--only` line ends with `path= level= picks= pickups=` (only when a path was live), `deaths:` gains `by_path=`, and a `paths:` summary line follows. The chain line gets the same fields after `card=`.
  - **`check.ps1`'s `bot_s7_stub`** now expects `weapons=pistol:\d(,shotgun:\d)? path=auto level=2 picks=1 pickups=\d+`. There is no `bot_s8_draft` (D14).
  - **`tools/path_lines.gd`** prints `[path_lines] arsenal=0 auto=N survival=0 budget=250` and exits 1 over budget.
- **Tests:**
  - New `tests/gut/test_paths.gd` (39 tests at build, 41 after the review), including G6 and G7 by name.
  - `test_answers` takes M45a's unbuilt texts and the `[tag]` problem.
  - `test_weapons`: the give at 24 with its say; the M44 switch line is now inline data.
  - `test_stage2_door`: the stub carries `path auto`.
  - `test_dash_aim`: the spacing tests include `path` cues.
  - The mutation-id lint needs no `paths/` skip while `offer_pool_extra = []`. **M45b must skip `res://paths/`** as it skips `weapons/`.
- **Known changes for later:** `warn_line` is the fallback warning; per-cue warnings come with S7 (M47). `camping` is kept on every path (D16).
- **Numbers:**
  - **G1:** v6, 4 runs, 0 failures. **G2:** gauntlet, 4/0. **G3:** campaign vs m43.3, 16/0. **G4:** S4 vs m42.2, 4/0. All four were run after the player/main.gd steps and again on the final tree.

Over the 16 runs, pickups have a median of 279.5 (262-291), kills 294.5 (284-298) and gems 124 (review fix: the first figures, 278 and 293, were medians of the four shard medians). The meter saturates (the 10-pick stop) well before 180 s.

A first check run failed on one "5 resources still in use at exit" line. The cause was a test lambda that captured its own LevelMeter (a RefCounted cycle). It was fixed in the test; the runtime meter's callables are bound to the PathRunner node.

The real-save snapshot also passed. export.ps1 gave **EXPORT PASSED** (PatchNotes.exe 104.9 MB).

The no-path pistol reads `Tuning.shoot_cooldown` 0.18 and `projectile_speed` 900, which equal `pistol.tres`'s rank-1 values. The fire paths also agree, so the flip is invisible by construction (M44 proved the same gate with the knockback sentinel).

Bite: a pick that logs `apply` fails it (11 vs 1).

New `test_the_auto_bot_goes_to_the_gate_before_a_gem` (fails with the old order). No hash moves: Stage 1 and the v6 loop never use the auto policy.

Bite: a pick that calls `Telemetry.append` fails it in 6 tests. `_arena()` also awaits a frame after `set_input`, so the replaced HumanInput no longer shows as an orphan.
