### 6.6 As built (M45b): Auto at scale
Auto holds its crowd. Path sessions get spatial-hash separation and split projectile budgets (player 140 / enemy 60), and `nova` and `well` exist at ranks 1-3. Stage 1 and the legacy loop keep the O(n²) separation and the combined cap of 200, statement for statement. Both v6 hashes, the m43.3 campaign hash, S4 vs m42.2 and every Stage 1 `WorldRules` field are unchanged. `BUILD = "M45b"`.
- **The one predicate:** `RunState.path_live()` = `live_path() != &""` (D1). It is true in the S8 draft from frame 0, in the S7 stub once its `path auto` swap lands (measured: game time 14.52 s, the 13 s cue plus the 1.5 s warning), with F2 PATH and in the bench. The real three-version S7 (not built) is covered by construction: it has no path before its `path` cue.
- **Spatial hash** (`entities/separation_grid.gd`, `SeparationGrid`, static, shared engine work):
  - Cells are `Tuning.enemy_separation_radius` (36 px), keyed by `floor(pos / cell)`.
  - It is rebuilt once per (physics frame, `RunState.world` reference) from the `enemy` group, in group order. `active()` clears it when no path is live.
  - `push()` reads the 3×3 cells with the legacy math on live positions (untyped iteration, `is_instance_valid` first).
  - `EnemyCommon.separation()` calls it only when active. The old loop body moved unchanged into `EnemyCommon.separation_all()`.
  - No RNG, no instance ids (D11). The grid push equals the legacy push within 1e-4 on 4 layouts, including coincident pairs, cell borders, beyond-radius pairs and a layout straddling x = 0 / y = 0.
- **Split budgets** (`RunState._under_cap`, `Tuning.player_projectile_budget = 140`, `enemy_projectile_budget = 60`): a path session counts each kind's own live + pending against its own budget. Otherwise the combined branch is unchanged.
- **Refusal counters:** `RunState.enemy_shots_refused` / `player_shots_refused`, counted in every mode (a counter changes no play; D9) and zeroed by `reset()`.
  - They appear in the campaign line (`CampaignDB.telemetry_record`; additive, `CAMPAIGN_SCHEMA` stays 4) and in the run line (`Telemetry.build_record`; additive, `SCHEMA` stays 3).
  - The bot's `paths:` summary ends with `enemy_shots_refused median=.. max=.. player_shots_refused median=..`. The per-seed `--only` line is unchanged (check.ps1's anchored regex). `run_log`, F1, `state_text()` and the debug overlay are untouched (out of scope).
- **`well_field.gd`** (v6 file, the D15 pattern as orbit at M44): `life` / `radius` / `pull` are vars defaulting to the consts, and `_after_pull(delta)` is a no-op hook. The legacy `well` patch is unchanged (G1/G2 after this step: 0 failures; test `test_the_v6_well_patch_field_is_unchanged`).
- **Nova and well** (`weapons/`, shared weapon data and code, no `# path:` tag; D2 below):
  - `WeaponDef.node_script` (D3): a passive's node script, "" = the orbit shards. `problems()` checks that it exists and is on a passive. `Loadout._attach` builds that node with `configure(w)`, then the traits' `passive_ready`; the orbit branch is verbatim.
  - **`nova`** (`nova.tres`, `nova_pulse.gd`): every 3 s it fires an `Explosion.blast` at 1.5 T, damage 3 (D4), kind `blast`, credited `weapon:nova`, knockback 0, never pushing the player. Its own timer runs on the physics delta.
    - Rank 2 `shove`: survivors within the radius get `Mover.knockback` of `SHOVE_IMPULSE` **610**. Calibrated in GUT: a chaser moves 46.7 px ≈ 1 T. The map's starting guess of 840 moved it 91 px: knockback distance grows faster than linearly.
    - Rank 3 `dash_nova`: `player_dashed` fires it now and restarts the 3 s timer, and the dash trigger then waits 3 s (D5).
    - Cards: "Nova: a blast around you every 3 s." / "Nova II: it shoves survivors away." / "Nova III: a dash sets it off early." Pitch: "Every few seconds, you go off. Politely."
    - Its look is the shared explosion feedback (burst, `shake(3.0)`, the look-gated flash, the sound): no new drawing (D12). **Feel risk:** a camera shake every 3 s (the owner can judge; muting needs a flag on `blast`, not done).
    - A rank-up rebuilds the node (`_rank_to` → `_attach`), so the 3 s timer and the dash wait restart then (accepted; orbit rebuilds the same way).
  - **`well`** (`well.tres`, `well_weapon.gd`, `well_weapon_field.gd` extends the v6 field): each player-faction kill (the gems' filter) leaves a field for 1.5 s (radius 2.6 T, pull 230; D6).
    - Rank 2 `gem_magnet` drags gems to the centre.
    - Rank 3 `teeth` deals 0.5 damage every 0.5 s (kind `burn`, `weapon:well`): 3 bites in a field's life, 1 a second. A bitten enemy shows its white hit flash.
    - Cards: "Well: kills leave a pull field." / "Well II: fields pull gems too." / "Well III: fields bite (1 dmg/s)."
    - A bomber's fuse blast (faction `enemy`) leaves no field. A bomber the player killed blasts as the player (`enemy_bomber.gd` `_death_faction`), so its blast kills **do** leave fields, just as they drop gems.
    - **At most 12 live fields (R6's fallback, taken; below).**
  - Trait ids are `shove`, `dash_nova`, `gem_magnet` and `teeth`. The 1.4 roster keeps the player-facing names ("magnet", "teeth"). Only the id differs, because `magnet` is a v6 mutation id.
- **Offered on Auto:** `paths/auto.tres` sets `offer_pool_extra = [nova, well]`. The mutation-id lint now skips **only `res://paths/*.tres`** (path data never touches `RunState.apply` / `MutationDB`), so `path_runner.gd`, `pick_offer.gd` and `paths/auto/*.gd` stay linted.
  - No session announces them yet: S9 and S11's `[nova]` / `[well]` says come at M48 / M51 (D10). Until then they are reached through F2 WEAPONS (it lists `WeaponDB.all()`), `--loadout=`, the bench and tests. The pool rule holds: never offered before a tagged say, and never as a third passive (`max_passives` 2).
- **Bench** (`tools/bench.gd`, tool code):
  - `--auto[=<enemies>]` (default 57, the S8 final-wave cap = "Auto at its enemy cap"; D7) and `--passives=<id,id>` (default `nova,well`).
  - Setup: `session_path = auto`, the runner frozen then begun, pistol / shotgun / laser / launcher at 3 plus the passives at 3.
  - Each tick it tops up enemies to N, gems to 60, and marked synthetic shots to `clamp(116 − live weapon shots, 0, 100)`, so the real weapons keep firing.
  - One `[bench] auto` line with `weapon_shots=` / `synthetic=` per-frame averages, `fields=avg/max`, `kills`, `game_t` and both refusal counters.
  - `--weapons=all:<r>` now picks `orbit` by id as the rider (well would otherwise have become "the last passive").
- **Tests:** the new `tests/gut/test_auto_scale.gd` has **21** tests: G6 and its control, the combined cap, 140/60, the one-frame 16-shot burst, shotgun r3 with every slot, grid = legacy, path-only builds, nova ×3, well ×7 (including the 12-field cap, the cap across a rank-up and the v6 patch field unchanged), the pool, telemetry, and a 15 s replay.
  - `test_weapons.gd`'s `IDS` gains nova and well.
  - `test_mutations.gd`'s lint skips `paths/*.tres`.
- **Deviations:**
  - **D2 (named deviation, owner question):** nova and well are built as shared weapon code in `weapons/` (no `# path:auto` tag), so `path_lines.gd` still says **auto=182**. Counted as Auto-only, their 7 scripts add **133** lines (nova_pulse 61, well_weapon 31, well_weapon_field 29, 4 traits × 3), which puts Auto at **315 > 250**. 1.5's ~150 for Auto never included them. **Question for the owner:** "nova/well counted as Auto-only puts Auto at 315 lines (> 250): accept them as shared weapons, raise the budget, or trim?" The grid and the budgets are engine work either way.
  - **R6 fallback taken, partly (D8):**
    - The first `--auto` run with nova + well measured **fps=1, physics avg 140.71 / p95 242.56 / max 267.18 ms**. The cause was a runaway: the bench's synthetic shots kill ~280 enemies a second, each kill's hit stop slows game time (`game_t` 2.8 s over 720 ticks at 30 enemies), and teeth kills leave more fields: **1852 live fields on average, 3374 at most**.
    - The fallback's third step, **well's live fields capped at 12** (`well_weapon.gd` `MAX_FIELDS`; a kill over the cap leaves none; the live list sits on `RunState.world` (meta `well_weapon_fields`), so a rank-up's rebuilt node still counts the old fields; tests `test_well_fields_stop_at_12_live`, `test_well_cap_holds_across_a_rank_up`), fixes it alone.
    - The enemy-cap and HP steps were **not** applied. The diagnosis points at the fields, and lower HP would only add kills. If they were ever needed, "HP ×0.8" would read as a multiplier on Auto's overlay (`enemy_hp_mult` 0.7 → 0.56), not as setting it to 0.8.
    - In real play the cap is rarely reached: S8 runs ~1.6 kills/s, about 2-3 live fields.
  - **G3's bite does not bite** (reported, not hidden): with `SeparationGrid.active()` forced true everywhere, G3 gave **16 runs, 0 failures**. Stage 1's crowds are thin, and the grid sums the same neighbours: with 2 neighbours the order cannot matter (a+b = b+a), so only ≥ 3 in-radius neighbours can differ in the last bit. The same bite on the denser v6 loop fails, so the hashes do catch it there: **G1 4 runs, 4 failures** (diverging at 91-119 s) and **G2 4 runs, 2 failures**. Reverted, not committed. The path-only predicate itself is pinned by `test_the_spatial_hash_runs_only_in_path_sessions` (builds unchanged in the legacy loop, in s1_06 and in the stub before its swap; > 0 in the draft and after the swap).
  - The S7 stub becomes a path session at game time 14.52 s (13 s cue + 1.5 s warning). The test asserts game time ≥ 13 s, not a frame count, because GUT's `wait_physics_frames(1)` advances 2 ticks here.
  - Not built (out of scope): rank-4 evolutions (M50.1), S9 / S11 content and their tagged says (M48 / M51), looks (the bench runs tier 0), any F1 / F2 change, any check.ps1 bench step. **The sniper and the boss ring are re-tested against the split budgets at M49 / M50** (PLAN 5 "Budgets"). This row names the v6 shooter, and G6 uses it.
- **Gates (all from `.check/ref_m45b/`):**
  - **G1** v6 compare: **4 runs, 0 failures**, after Steps 2-3, after Step 4 and on the final tree.
  - **G2** gauntlet compare: **4 runs, 0 failures**, at the same three points.
  - **G3** campaign vs `ref_m43.3`: **16 runs, 0 failures** (after Steps 2-3 and final).

  - **G5:** `test_stage_1_rules_equal_the_m43_3_fixture_field_by_field` is green (429 asserts).
  - **G6 (hard):** `test_a_v6_shooter_still_fires_with_auto_saturated` is green. On Auto with 4 aimed weapons r3 + nova r3 + well r3, 70 held chasers and 140 frozen player shots topped up every tick, the v6 shooter's shot spawns within 3 s. The player count read at the frame it fired was **[140]**, `enemy_shots_refused` **0**, and `player_shots_refused` > 0 (the real weapons were refused: saturated). The control `test_without_a_path_the_combined_cap_starves_the_shooter` (no path, 200 frozen shots) is green: no enemy shot gets in and `enemy_shots_refused` > 0.
  - **G7:** the full GUT suite is **768/768** (748 + 20; 14661 asserts, 45 scripts, 161 s), with no SCRIPT ERROR / Parse Error / ERROR / resources-in-use lines.
  - **G8:** check.ps1 -BotRuns gave **CHECK PASSED** twice: 767/767 inside on the tree before the field cap, and **768/768** inside (14451 asserts) on the final tree.

### 6.7 As built (M46): Arsenal
Arsenal is playable as a draft (`s8_draft_arsenal`): a hand of up to 3 cards switched with Q / E, the wheel, the bumpers or keys 1-3, quick-draw, a card meter fed by kills, the HandWidget, and the `arsenal` bot policy with the flank rule (stub-tested). Stage 1 and the legacy loop do not move: both v6 hashes, the m43.3 campaign hash, S4 vs m42.2 and every Stage 1 `WorldRules` field are unchanged. The S7 stub stays an Auto taste and its line is unchanged. `BUILD = "M46"`.
- **References first (`.check/ref_m46/`):** on the M45b tree (a scratch worktree at 8f73984) `before_s7_3i.log` (`win t=60.0 ... weapons=pistol:1,shotgun:1 path=auto level=2 picks=1 pickups=32`) and the Auto S8 mortal baseline `auto_mortal/before_shard1-4.log` (median_t 132.5 s, as M45b R3). No new hash recording: G3 compares `ref_m43.3`, G4 `ref_m42.2`, tier 0 `ref_m45b`.
- **Data:**
  - `PathDef` gains `full_line` (the meter-full line, which replaces `PathRunner.FULL_LINE`; Auto's .tres carries the same text), `pick_line` (the panel's line, default "Level up. Pick one.") and `quick_draw_mult`. `problems()`: a `cards` path needs `level_thresholds.size() >= max_picks` (for cards they are **cumulative kills**), `full_line` must parse, `pick_line` must not be empty, and `quick_draw_mult >= 1`.
  - `paths/arsenal.tres`: `manual_one`, `cards`, start `[pistol]`, 3 slots, 1 passive (D5: nothing offers one in M46), `offer_pool_extra []`, every density ×1.0, widget `hand`, policy `arsenal`, basics `[cards, quick_draw]`, **`level_thresholds` 25, 60, 109, 178, 274, 408** (base 25, ×1.4; D1 retune, see Deviations), taste `[10]` (S7, M47), 6 picks, `quick_draw_mult` 2.0, warn "happy: New version. Your guns are cards.", full "thinking: That's enough cards for one test.", pick "New card. Pick one.".
  - `Unlocks.CAMPAIGN_BASICS` += `cards`, `quick_draw`. `Cue.UNBUILT_PATHS` = `{survival: M47}`. F2 PATH enables `arsenal`, and "+1 level" is now "+1 level/card".
  - `campaign/drafts/s8_draft_arsenal.tres` (outside `campaign.tres`, like `s8_draft`): S6's basics, chaser / shooter / `fork:fork_enemy`, spawn ×1.3 / cap ×1.3, `hp_floor 0`, survive 120, weapons pistol + shotgun. Its cues are `at 10: add cards` + "Remember the cards? Now they're real. Rank pips, a meter. Twenty-five kills, a new card.", `at 20: add quick_draw` + the quick-draw secret, and `at 30: give shotgun` + its line. Its debrief has the three 3.2 S8 (Arsenal) lines.
- **Runtime:**
  - **Keys 1-3** (`weapon_1..3`, physical KEY_1..3, the same keys as `choose_1..3`; no pad bind). `HumanInput.select_slot` is the first just-pressed of them, -1 while anything pauses play. `_wait_release` also waits for a held digit, and the tick that ends the wait clears `select_slot`. The player calls `Loadout.select_slot(i)` after `switch_step` (manual modes). `BotInput` resets `select_slot = -1` every tick.
  - **AZERTY labels (D2):** `InputSetup.number_row_label`: a number-row key whose layout label is not A-Z / 0-9 (`&`, `é`, `"`) prints its digit. This also fixes the pick panel's `[{key:choose_n}]` on AZERTY.
  - **`Loadout`:** `select_slot(i)` (out of range or already active: a no-op, so Stage 1's one pistol never moves), `switches` (fp), `quick_draw_mult` (reset by `apply_path`) and the quick-draw flag. A real change of the active slot (cycle or number) arms it, and the next shot that fires spends it (no timeout, D4). The legacy pistol branch is untouched.
  - **Quick-draw** (`paths/arsenal/quick_draw.gd`, `QuickDraw.mult`): ×2 once armed and live, logged as `quick_draw` in `run_log`. The multiplier goes into the pellet params through `Shooter.fire(..., damage_mult := 1.0)`, so traits' `shot`/`spawned` and `projectile_fired` see ×2. It is live only after `add quick_draw` (D3).
  - **The card meter** (`paths/arsenal/card_meter.gd`): it counts player-faction kills (the gems' filter; a bomber's own fuse kill does not count) from the moment `cards` is added. Each cumulative threshold deals a card (`want_pick`). It stops after 6 cards with `full_line`, and a tryout stops quietly after its taste list.
  - **`PathRunner`:**
    - `_build_cards` runs with the same guards as `_build_levels` (`_meter == null`, `not frozen`, the basic live). The `quick_draw` basic sets `Loadout.quick_draw_mult`.
    - The `enemy_killed` feed is connected only while a card meter exists. `_clear_growth` (swap / F2), `freeze()` and `_exit_tree` disconnect it.
    - `level()` and `gems()` read only a levelups meter (Arsenal reports level 1 and gems 0). New `cards()`, `kills_to_next()` and `fingerprint()`.
    - `force_level()` fills the next card's kills. F1 shows `path=arsenal cards=n kills=k/need picks=n pickups=n`.
    - On cards, `_open_offer` puts `most_fired(lo)` first (the held aimed card with the most shots that can still rank up; ties go to slot order; D7). `PickOffer.draw` sets `offer.line = def.pick_line`.
    - `give()` is unchanged (false on cards → SessionRunner hands a free card, the weakest non-active replaced on a full hand).
    - After a pick, the player's input gets `require_fresh_press()`, so the key that picked card n never also picks slot n.
    - The crowd overlay is built only when `crowd` is in the path's own basics (D6).
  - **`HandWidget`** (`paths/arsenal/hand_widget.gd`, the LevelWidget corner style): one row per aimed card, `[{key:weapon_n}] Pistol ●○○`, the active row raised 6 px and brighter, then "cards n/6" and a thin bar to the next card. It has no passive row until M51.
  - **The fingerprint** (`paths/fingerprint.gd`, shared, report-only, 1.15) is sampled on PathRunner's physics tick: fire_share, picks / switches / pickups per min, travel_tps, mean nearest-enemy distance (every 6 ticks) and ammo_dry_share 0. `menu_s` is wall time from offer to pick (the tree is paused meanwhile). It is not written to telemetry (D9, M47 adds it).
- **Bot and tools:**
  - `BotInput._arsenal_policy` (shared bot code). It moves, aims and fires like the default policy; the default branch is unchanged (G1/G2). It wants the shotgun with an enemy < 4 T, the laser with ≥ 3 enemies in a 0.5 T corridor to 12 T, and the launcher with ≥ 3 enemies within its blast of `AutoFire.densest`'s node (aimed there; a cross-path call into `paths/auto/`, R5). Otherwise it wants the highest rank. It holds a choice 30 ticks and switches through `select_slot`.
  - **Flank rule:** a target with `shield_arc_deg` > 0 whose arc around `facing` covers the bot is stepped around 90° (the shorter way out), with fire held. `BotInput.flank_step` is static and tested on a stub.
  - `tools/bot_runs.gd`:
    - `--probe=no_switch|first_pick`. `first_pick` takes card 0 but still consumes its own `pick_rng` draw (`pick_card`). Offers stay paired with the default run only while both hands match: `PickOffer.draw` rolls `randf` once per card after `first`, so once the hands differ (offer 3 or 4 on the draft) the candidate counts and the stream diverge. R4's first_pick numbers are therefore an unpaired comparison.
    - On Arsenal, the `--only` line ends with `cards=n offers=@t,... cards_fired=k shots=<id:pellets>`, and an `[botrun] arsenal:` summary follows (offers, gaps, per-seed mean gap, tails, in_band, cards_fired>=2).
    - **`--fp`** (off by default) adds per-seed `fp=` at the end of each path line, a `[botrun] fp:` summary per path and `t_list=` on `deaths:`. check.ps1 never passes it.
  - `path_lines.gd` header updated.
- **Tests:** the new `tests/gut/test_arsenal.gd` has **32** tests (30 at build, 2 added by the review) covering data, built/unbuilt, binds and AZERTY, select_slot, pause / fresh-press / tap, quick-draw ×4 (as spawned, traits, no boost on Auto / no path / before the basic / legacy, default mult exact), the card meter ×5 (feeds and disconnects included), offers, give, HandWidget, G6, G7, the bot ×5 (shotgun + hysteresis + laser, launcher 3-vs-2, no_switch, first_pick, the flank stub), a 15 s replay, power-stays, the draft's audit rows and fp. `test_paths` and `test_answers` take the built Arsenal.

- **Review (3 findings, 3 upheld, all fixed):**
  - **`first_pick` was documented as paired with the default run; it is not.** `pick_card` keeps its own `pick_rng` draw, but `PickOffer.draw` rolls `randf` once per card after `first`, so once first_pick's hand differs from the default's (offer 3 or 4 on the draft) the candidate counts, `n` and the stream diverge. Fixed in the docs (the bot_runs header and `pick_card` comment, the BotInput header, Bot and tools and R4 above): only the pick draw is kept, and offers stay paired only while the hands match; R4's probe medians are unpaired. `PickOffer.draw` is unchanged (a fixed roll count would move Auto's stream). Doc-only, so no failing-first test.
  - **The scene-free part of `test_a_real_kill_reaches_the_meter_and_swaps_leave_no_feed` was vacuous.** It freed a frozen runner, which had no card feed, so `PathRunner._exit_tree`'s `_feed_cards(false)` was never exercised. The test now frees the scene with a live feed and asserts `before + 1` before `remove_child`, `before` right after it (the tree exit, before Godot's free-time auto-disconnect) and after `free()`. The freeze half moved to the new `test_freeze_drops_the_card_feed` (also: a frozen runner's `swap_now(&"arsenal")` builds no feed). With `_feed_cards(false)` removed from `_exit_tree` the test fails ("leaving the tree drops it": 1 vs 0).
  - **`QuickDraw.mult` logged `quick_draw` on shots refused at the projectile cap.** `mult()` is now pure; the new `QuickDraw.spent(loadout, m)` logs once, called by `Loadout.try_fire` only after `Shooter.fire` returned pellets, next to `_drawn = false`. New `test_a_quick_draw_refused_at_the_cap_logs_nothing_and_stays_armed` (player budget 0 for 3 ready ticks: no event, still armed; then the first spawned shot is x2 and logs exactly 1; the next logs nothing). With the old order (log before fire) it fails (3 vs 0, 4 vs 1).
  - **After the fixes:** full GUT **801/801** (46 scripts, 14720 asserts, 144 s; no SCRIPT ERROR / Parse Error / ERROR / resources-in-use lines). v6 compare 4 runs, 0 failures; gauntlet 4 runs, 0 failures. check.ps1 -BotRuns **CHECK PASSED** (801/801 inside, chain `play=225.8`, S7 stub line unchanged). export.ps1 **EXPORT PASSED** (104.9 MB). `path_lines` now reads `arsenal=130 auto=182 survival=0` (quick_draw 8 → 11). Logs: `.check/ref_m46/review/`.
- Nothing touched the owner's real save or `user://playtest/runs.jsonl`.
  - Bot runs used `--log=user://m46_*/...`, and GUT redirected `Telemetry.log_path` to `user://save_arsenal_test/runs.jsonl` (asserted never written).
  - Captures ran with `--inert-save`, and check.ps1's real-save snapshot passed.

- **Tier-0 decision (2026-11-24, at commit):** seed 2's pixel (685, 484) flips with the `BUILD` string alone (M45b tree + `BUILD = "M46"` reproduces it), so it is capture noise, not a game change. From M46 on, the tier-0 compare masks that one pixel; the baseline stays `.check/ref_m45b/tier0_*`.

### 6.8 As built (M47): Survival, the real S7, fork_path, S8 and the G14a build
Stage 2 now plays: S7 "Three games" swaps the whole shooter live (Arsenal, Survival, Auto), `fork_path` picks one, and S8 "Day one" plays it for real (no look try-on yet). Survival is built, the splitter is the first `EnemyVariant`, and the start screen replays S8 as any path. Stage 1 and the legacy loop do not move: both v6 hash compares and the campaign hashes have 0 failures.

- **Runtime:**
  - **Survival (D6-D10, D19):** one slot (the weapon, else the pistol sidearm, which never runs dry); drops from kills (10%, 35% from big enemies; weapon vs ammo 50/50 while holding a weapon; a pistol pickup only on the sidearm, and it ranks the sidearm up); crates (none in a tryout; 2 at `add pickups`, then 1 per 20 s, max 3, never expire); walk over a gun to take it, the old one drops at your feet and stays untouchable until the player leaves the take radius (min 0.5 s); ammo +50% of max; `kill_heal`; `battery` for the orbit; the `drop` cue (Survival only); `give` on Survival = a crate at the free pickup tile nearest the player. The `survival` bot policy walks the BFS field; `--probe=no_pickup` ignores pickups.
  - **Swaps:** `PathRunner.swap_now` frees player shots, the `pickup` group and the growth pieces (Drops, crate timer, kill-heal, ammo widget), and sweeps both groups again on the next physics frame (spawns are deferred), keeping the new path's own spawns (Survival's first crates). A frame later the leftovers check reports any shot or pickup either sweep took that is still alive, plus the growth / widget / feed / crowd / loadout checks; `path_leftovers` logs it with `late` (what the second sweep took). Per-cue warnings `path <id> [<mood>: <line>]` (D12); `path off` has none and ends the crowd overlay (D21).

The v6 mutation code is untouched; variants are never fork unlocks (D13).

  - **F1 had no Survival fields** although D25 said so. `PathRunner.state_text()` now has a Survival branch: `path=survival weapon=shotgun:2 ammo=18/24 picks=n pickups=n heals=n` (`ammo=-` on the sidearm). The bot line is a separate format and is unchanged. Test: `test_f1_shows_survivals_weapon_ammo_and_heals`.
  - **The Survival bot could swap two aimed guns back and forth** (walk away, the dropped gun turns touchable, walk back). `BotInput._would_bounce` now skips a weapon pickup that would swap out the aimed gun in hand while that gun has half its ammo or more, and it never goes back for the gun it just dropped. Pistol and passive pickups and same-gun rank-ups are unchanged. Shipped S7/S8 could not trigger the loop (their only aimed gun is the shotgun), and the S7/S8 hashes and the R1/R4 Survival numbers are unchanged. Test: `test_the_bot_never_bounces_between_two_aimed_guns`.
  - **The leftovers check could never fail for shots or pickups** (raised twice). It skipped nodes queued by the sweep that ran just before it. Now each sweep records what it took (weak refs). The second sweep runs on the next physics frame, counted in frames so a swap from F2 or a test also waits. The check runs one frame after that and reports any recorded shot or pickup that is still alive. `path_leftovers` logs `late` (what the second sweep took). Tests: `test_a_shot_and_a_kill_drop_on_the_swap_tick_are_gone_by_the_check` (both are freed, the check reads []) and `test_the_check_reports_them_when_the_second_sweep_frees_nothing` (the `late_sweep` hook off: the check names both groups).
  - **The second sweep deleted Survival's first two crates** on a cue swap outside a tryout. It now keeps the new path's own spawns (`Drops._mine`), passed as an untyped array: check.ps1 caught the first version calling `has()` on a typed array with a shot. Test: `test_a_cue_swap_to_survival_keeps_its_two_first_crates` (the swap comes from PathRunner's own tick, with a shot fired on that tick). It fails both without the keep and with the typed keep. The existing crates test also fails without it.
  - **Kill-heal counted heals at full HP.** `Drops._heal` counts and logs `kill_heal` only when HP actually rose. R1's "heals 21" were heal ticks: invincible S8 on Survival now reports heals 0 (0-0) over 16 seeds, and every other number is identical. Mortal S8 on Survival, 16 seeds: heals median 11.5 (4-16), against 20 (11-22) heal ticks before; lose 5/16 as before. The telemetry `kill_heal` now means HP restored. Test: `test_kill_heal_counts_from_its_arming_and_never_on_auto` (the 5th kill at full HP counts 0; on Auto the HP stays down after 10 kills).
  - **Tests that passed without their behaviour, and S7 tests the spec named that were missing.** Fixed: the minis test asserts 2 minis. S8's kill-heal test emits 10 real kills and checks heals 0 at kill 9 and 1 at kill 10. Auto's "no kill-heal" now reads HP, not `heals()`. `test_the_stop_ends_s7_at_152_s` moves the clock and checks the stop at 150, still playing just before 152 and `stopped` at 152.0. Added: `test_each_swap_wipes_once_and_crossfades_under_reduce_flashing` (at most 3 wipes a second over the four landings, one wipe per swap via `PathRunner.wipes`, the fade and no glitch under reduce flashing), `test_the_arsenal_taste_deals_one_card_after_10_kills`, `test_the_auto_taste_levels_once_with_no_passive_offered`, `test_the_survival_taste_drops_and_heals_with_no_crate`, the 35% big-enemy rate (as a ratio to the 10% rate) in the loot_rng drop test, and pickup-tile `blocked` / `unreachable` problems. These are new coverage: they pass on the built code.

### 6.9 As built (M47.2): G14a feedback
The owner's three G14a notes (2026-11-25, on the M47 build): "mascot on the right hide UI => put UI to the top right"; "cards should have a card hand on bottom left and you see current weapons and which is equipped (real cards)"; "survival 2:00 => 1:30". No pair scores were given, so M47.1 does not run. The legacy v6 loop and its HUD do not move (both v6 compares 0 failures); Stage 1 plays the same hashes; on screen only the mascot's corner position moved in Stage 1.
- **References (`.check/ref_m47_2/`):** `before_s8_{arsenal,auto,survival}.png` (the M47 tree, taken from a `git archive m47` copy, since the build was already in the working tree), `s8_survival_hash.jsonl` (the new Survival S8 ref, D14), `tier0_1..5.png` (the new tier-0 baseline), the capture script `capture_s8.gd` and every gate log.
- **Data:**
  - `SessionDef.goal_by_path: Dictionary[StringName, float]` (path id -> goal value) and `goal_for(path_id)`. `problems()`: an unknown or non-StringName key, a value <= 0, on a pathless session, on a tryout, and (survive / stop goals) any `at s` cue that can run on path p after that path's end (survive: s >= goal; stop: s > goal + `Tuning.smoke_stop_read`; S0 and S1 stay clean). Conditions are read per path (`_cue_can_run_on`: a positive `answer:path_<other>` or a `!answer:path_<p>` part rules a cue out on p).
  - `s2_02_day_one.tres`: `goal_by_path = {&"survival": 90.0}` (typed), `goal_value` stays 120; the intro's last line split into `!answer:path_survival` "Two minutes. I'll build it properly this time." and `answer:path_survival` "Ninety seconds. I'll build it properly this time." (D8). No cue changes (all <= 50 s). `lines_stage2.md` regenerated.
- **Runtime:**
  - `TopdownGenre.begin_run` reads `session.goal_for(live path)` before the kills multiplier, so the goal text shows "Survive 1:30" on Survival (replay-as-path too); the session-end telemetry's goal value is 90 (tested). `Reactions._survive_left()` reads the run loop's goal (D9): `last_seconds` fires at 85-90 s on Survival.
  - **The HUD band (`ui/hud.gd`, campaign only):** `BAND_TOP` 8, `BAND_BOTTOM` 68, `BAND_GAP` 18, `PATH_MAX_W` 300; `corner_rect(view)` is a fixed reserve measured from the widest corner text (`CORNER_WIDEST`: "HP 999   v9.99.99", "teleport ready"), so the band widget never jitters with HP or the dash line; `goal_rect`, `band_rect`. `layout_path_widget(view)` runs from `set_path_widget` and in the session branch of `_process` (never in v6): a widget with `hand_rect()` places itself, anything else is right-aligned in the band at its minimum size. `_label`'s own placement and the v6 branch are unchanged.
  - **LevelWidget (Auto):** "LV n" with a 120x6 bar beside it, the held list on one line at font 15, "... +n" past 300 px (`shown_held()`; `text()` and F1 keep the whole list). **AmmoWidget (Survival):** font 17, its minimum size is its text; one line shorter (G15).
  - **The mascot (D1, measured):** at `TOP` 64 the resting cursor reached y 59.5 and the window y 63.2 (blob 70.4, caret 97.8), inside the 2-line corner text (y 8-67), and the worst unclamped poses reached y 16-22. Branches (c) and (b) together: `Mascot.TOP` 76 (resting tops 71.5 / 75.2 / 82.4 / 109.8, all below 68), and `MascotBody.top_limit` (set by the corner only while the layer is unshifted, so the stage card keeps its whole pose) pushes a hop, pop or pointing lean down by its overshoot: the clamped worst top is 69.0 for cursor, window and blob (caret 99.2). `MascotBody.outline()` per body, `posed()` / `posed_bounds()` / `worst_bounds()` (every expression × rest + 8 pointing directions × glitch on / off × expr_t 0 / 0.35 × 2 s at 1/30 s), `Mascot.keep_out(view)` and `Mascot.bubble_rect(text, view)`. The bubble follows (`TOP + 14` = 90). The stage card places its centred AI relative to `Mascot.TOP`, so it does not move.
  - **The hand (`paths/arsenal/hand_widget.gd`, D4, D10):** bottom left, 3 reserved slots of 64x92 cards (gap 8, margin 8; at 720 the resting top is 620, the equipped card raised 8 to 612), the card meter ("cards n/6" at font 14 over a 110x6 bar) to their right. Each card is drawn: a rounded `StyleBoxFlat` (corner 6) on a dark face, a 3 px `LIT` border on the equipped card, 2 px `DIM` at alpha 0.8 on the others, the key badge (`InputSetup.expand_keys("{key:weapon_n}")`), the title (shrunk to fit, 13 to 8 pt), `Loadout.MAX_RANK` pips and the weapon glyph in the lower half. The switch raise takes 0.12 s and a join slides up 40 px and fades in over 0.25 s (`move_toward` in `_process`; `manual` + `step()` for tests); the join's brightness flash is 0 under reduce flashing, and the lit card never pulses. It runs under pause (Hud's ALWAYS), so a card from a pick slides in behind the panel. `text()`, `bar()` and `active_row()` are kept (F1, M46 tests). `ui/weapon_glyph.gd` (`WeaponGlyph.draw`: the 7 `WeaponDef.icon` keys + a "?" fallback; keys only, never weapon ids).
  - `tools/bot_runs.gd`: `--session-set <id>.goal_value=X` also clears that copy's `goal_by_path` (`set_session_field`, prints `[botrun] <id>: goal_value=X replaces goal_by_path`). `tools/check.ps1`: S8 Survival asserts `outcome=win t=90.0`, the others 120.0.

  - `test_reduce_flashing_never_pulses_the_hand` samples `lift`, `joining` and `join_flash` (what the draw reads), not the drawn modulate / border colours. The toast test checks the stage-set toasts (the smoke and dev toasts are Stage 1 / legacy only).
